JARVIS EXACT SOURCE INSTALL — not a design prompt. Read INSTALL.md and SKILL.md. Install these exact source files, preserving the student account configuration. Do not redraw or replace components. Secrets and business records are deliberately absent.


===== FILE: INSTALL.md =====
# Jarvis exact source handoff

This is a source handoff, not a tested fresh-account auto-installer. The visual and behavior must come from the supplied `source/` tree; do not redraw, reinterpret, simplify, or replace components with a design prompt.

## Facilitator sequence

1. Back up the student’s existing app/repository and record its current Base44 account/app configuration, `.env` files, data, routes and workflows. Preserve them; do not overwrite or merge blindly.
2. Run `configure.mjs` from this extracted package BEFORE copying its `source/` tree. Supply only the student's own non-secret IDs/name/bot URL. It substitutes three configuration-bearing files without changing the design. Example: `node configure.mjs --hud-app-id STUDENT_HUD_ID --superagent-id STUDENT_AGENT_ID --owner-name "Student Name" --telegram-url https://t.me/STUDENT_BOT --timezone Australia/Brisbane`. The IDs must be real 24-character Base44 IDs; omit the Telegram argument to leave its button disabled. Never pass a token or password.
3. Copy the configured `source/` tree faithfully into the student project, reviewing collisions first. Keep the exact `src/pages/Today.jsx` route at `/`, `src/components/jarvis/Reactor.jsx`, `Reactor.css`, `src/index.css`, `tailwind.config.js`, `src/components/`, `base44/`, and runtime configuration. Preserve the student's app binding and merge entity schemas rather than overwriting unrelated entities.
4. Configure the student’s own Base44 runtime app/environment values. Configure backend secrets only in the student’s server-side secret store. Do not copy reference data, tokens, keys, phone values or account credentials.
5. Verify the generated tree with the supplied `verify.mjs` and root hash manifest. Stop on any mismatch; do not “fix” differences by redesigning source.
6. Install the included `facilitator/setupJarvisControls/` as the temporary backend function `setupJarvisControls`. Follow `facilitator/BOOTSTRAP-REFERENCE.md` for initialization/enrollment semantics, not its older external generator command. The exact runtime source is already included here. Use `facilitator/after-call-workflow-DISABLED.json` as the five-minute workflow draft. Keep it disabled until secret, control rows, auth and timestamp checks pass. Brisbane-only call runtime. Remove the temporary bootstrap after verified setup.
7. Do not publish, place calls, enable outbound calling, or claim fresh-account proof in this handoff.

## Student builder upload prompt (copy exactly; one prompt)

```text
Use the supplied Jarvis source tree exactly as provided. Do not redraw, reinterpret, simplify, or replace the design. Preserve the existing student app’s account configuration, environment, data and workflows. Mount src/pages/Today.jsx at / and retain the exact Reactor.jsx, Reactor.css, index.css, tailwind config, components, Base44 entities/functions and runtime config. Use configure.mjs for student-owned non-secret IDs/name/channel values, then verify with verify.mjs and the root hash manifest. Do not publish or place calls.
```

## Boundaries

The exact source comes from the export, not from prose. This does not prove a fresh-account setup, native-route stability, voice quality, automatic EOD calls, Telegram delivery or production readiness. Retell is not part of this clone.

If the builder cannot inspect a ZIP, attach the included `DASHBOARD-SOURCE.txt` instead. It contains the actual source, not a design description. If the tool cannot access all files, STOP and report the limitation; never recreate missing code from memory or a screenshot. Build the copied project with `npm install` and `npm run build` in the target environment before publication; the source-only checks here do not replace that build.


===== FILE: SKILL.md =====
---
name: jarvis-exact-dashboard
description: Install the supplied Jarvis dashboard source faithfully; do not generate or redesign the dashboard from prose.
---

# Exact Jarvis clone handoff skill

Use this skill only when installing the supplied exact source clone for a student. The source tree is authoritative. Do not invent replacement JSX, CSS, entities, backend functions, routes, workflow definitions or visual behavior from a description.

## Required order

1. Read `INSTALL.md` and inspect the entire supplied `source/` tree.
2. Back up the student app and preserve its account configuration, environment, data, workflows and existing integrations.
3. Prepare the source faithfully, including the complete `src/components/` tree, `src/pages/Today.jsx`, `src/components/jarvis/Reactor.jsx`, `Reactor.css`, `src/index.css`, `tailwind.config.js`, `vite.config.js`, `base44/` entities/functions/shared code, and runtime app configuration.
4. Run the supplied `configure.mjs` BEFORE copying the configured source into the target app, with explicit non-secret substitutions only:
   - `__STUDENT_HUD_APP_ID__`
   - `__STUDENT_SUPERAGENT_ID__`
   - `__STUDENT_TELEGRAM_URL__`
   - `__STUDENT_OWNER_NAME__`
5. Keep the student’s own Base44 environment/app configuration. Backend secrets belong only in the student’s server-side secret store; never render or log them.
6. Run the supplied `verify.mjs` and compare the root hash manifest. Any mismatch is a stop condition.
7. Treat the facilitator bootstrap and draft five-minute native after-call workflow as a separate, disabled setup. Enable only after its own auth, secret, control-row and timestamp checks. Call runtime is Australia/Brisbane only.

## Faithfulness rules

- Do not use a design prompt to recreate the page.
- Do not redraw the reactor or substitute another component library.
- Do not remove “unused” files, alter routes, normalize formatting, or refactor source before verification.
- Do not overwrite the student’s existing full schema map, data, `.env`, workflows or account configuration.
- Never copy reference account IDs, records, tokens, keys, phone numbers or bot URLs. `configure.mjs` is the only permitted non-secret substitution path.
- Do not add Retell or claim EOD automatic calls.

## Stop conditions

Stop and report the exact file/error if the source archive, `configure.mjs`, `verify.mjs` or root hash manifest is missing; if any substitution input is missing; if the student timezone is not Australia/Brisbane; if backup/preservation is unavailable; or if verification hashes differ. Do not reconstruct missing source from prose.

## Handoff status

This process produces a source/configuration handoff. It does not publish, place a call, enable outbound calling, or establish fresh-account success. Any later live test must be separately recorded with student-owned evidence.


===== FILE: configure.mjs =====
// Mechanical account substitutions only. Does not deploy, call, or handle secrets.
import fs from 'node:fs';
import path from 'node:path';
import {fileURLToPath} from 'node:url';
const root=path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
const arg=n=>{const i=process.argv.indexOf('--'+n);return i<0?undefined:process.argv[i+1]};
const values={__STUDENT_HUD_APP_ID__:arg('hud-app-id'),__STUDENT_SUPERAGENT_ID__:arg('superagent-id'),__STUDENT_OWNER_NAME__:arg('owner-name'),__STUDENT_TELEGRAM_URL__:arg('telegram-url')||''};
function fail(m){console.error(m);process.exit(1)}
for(const k of ['__STUDENT_HUD_APP_ID__','__STUDENT_SUPERAGENT_ID__'])if(!/^[a-f0-9]{24}$/.test(values[k]||''))fail('Supply both student Base44 IDs; no teacher IDs.');
if(!/^[\p{L}][\p{L} -]{0,79}$/u.test(values.__STUDENT_OWNER_NAME__||''))fail('Owner name must use letters, spaces or hyphens.');
if(values.__STUDENT_TELEGRAM_URL__&&!/^https:\/\/t\.me\/[A-Za-z0-9_]+$/.test(values.__STUDENT_TELEGRAM_URL__))fail('Telegram must be the student bot HTTPS t.me URL, or omitted.');
if(arg('timezone')!=='Australia/Brisbane')fail('This exact snapshot has Brisbane date arithmetic. Other timezones require separate tested changes.');
const files=['src/components/jarvis/HeaderActions.jsx','base44/functions/callMe/entry.ts','base44/shared/after-call-bridge.ts'];
const planned=files.map(p=>{const f=path.join(root,'source',p);let c=fs.readFileSync(f,'utf8');if(!c.includes('__STUDENT_'))fail('Already configured: use a fresh archive; refusing to guess old values.');for(const [k,v]of Object.entries(values))c=c.split(k).join(v);return[f,c]});
for(const[f,c]of planned)fs.writeFileSync(f,c);
console.log('Configured 3 account-specific source files. No deployment, secret changes or calls. Keep native calling disabled until facilitator checks pass.');


===== FILE: verify.mjs =====
import fs from 'node:fs';
import path from 'node:path';
import {createHash} from 'node:crypto';
import {fileURLToPath} from 'node:url';
const root=path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
const manifest=JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(path.join(root,'MANIFEST.json'),'utf8'));
const hash=s=>createHash('sha256').update(s).digest('hex');
let exact=0;
for(const item of manifest.files){
 const filename=path.join(root,'source',item.path);if(!fs.existsSync(filename))throw Error('Missing '+item.path);
 const source=fs.readFileSync(filename,'utf8');
 if(!item.account_configuration){if(hash(source)!==item.sha256)throw Error('Changed source '+item.path);exact++}
 if(/6a8a73a121ef691355f714c9|6aab5cd44fd7225657336376|b44_9ed4b58a1bf40923_bot/.test(source))throw Error('Reference account leaked into '+item.path);
 if(/(?:sk_live_|ghp_|xoxb-)[A-Za-z0-9_-]{15,}|eyJhbGci[A-Za-z0-9_.-]{30,}|-----BEGIN (?:RSA )?PRIVATE KEY-----/.test(source))throw Error('Possible secret '+item.path);
 if(/\.(?:jsx?|tsx?)$/.test(item.path))for(const m of source.matchAll(/(?:from\s*|import\s*)['"](@\/[^'"]+|\.{1,2}\/[^'"]+)['"]/g)){
  const target=m[1].split('?')[0];const base=target.startsWith('@/')?path.join(root,'source/src',target.slice(2)):path.resolve(path.dirname(filename),target);
  if(!['','.js','.jsx','.ts','.tsx','.css','/index.js','/index.jsx','/index.ts'].some(e=>fs.existsSync(base+e)))throw Error('Missing import '+item.path+' -> '+target);
 }
}
console.log(JSON.stringify({files:manifest.files.length,lockedFilesVerified:exact,accountFiles:manifest.files.filter(f=>f.account_configuration).map(f=>f.path),imports:'resolved',referenceAccounts:'absent',note:'Source integrity, not fresh-account deployment or call proof.'},null,2));

===== FILE: facilitator/BOOTSTRAP-REFERENCE.md =====
# Facilitator package generator (native-call pilot)

This directory generates a student-specific source bundle from the reviewed local HUD/native-call snapshots. It does **not** deploy, publish, create credentials, create a Superagent, or prove fresh-account setup. The native route is an observed internal Base44 route with no stability guarantee. Retell is intentionally excluded.

Run the local bootstrap mocks before handing off:

```sh
node student-jarvis-pilot/facilitator-package/test-setupJarvisControls.mjs
```

The mocks do not call Base44, a phone service or the network.

## Generate a bundle

From the course workspace:

```sh
node student-jarvis-pilot/facilitator-package/generate.mjs \
  --hud-app-id <student-HUD-app-id> \
  --superagent-id <student-Superagent-id> \
  --owner-name "Student name" \
  --timezone Australia/Brisbane \
  --out /private/tmp/jarvis-student-bundle
```

All five inputs are required. IDs must be Base44 24-character lowercase hex IDs. This reviewed package is deliberately restricted to `Australia/Brisbane`: the current verified helper uses Brisbane-specific UTC+10 date arithmetic, so another timezone is a blocker rather than a string substitution. The generator fails before writing if an input is missing or invalid. It never accepts or emits a token, phone number, existing record, or reference account ID.

## Manual setup (facilitator, per student)

1. Log into Base44 with the student’s account and confirm the app is private/login-required. Do not invite the student to the reference HUD.
2. Review the generated entity/function files against the student app. **Merge missing fields and entities; never overwrite the student’s existing full entity schema map.** Preserve existing business logic, RLS, Telegram handlers and workflows. Resolve any schema conflict manually and record it.
3. Install the generated backend functions, shared bridge, and `HeaderActions.jsx` in the student HUD app. Replace `__STUDENT_TELEGRAM_URL__` only with the student’s own approved channel or remove that button; never copy a reference bot URL. Confirm owner/admin authentication and service-role writes before enabling any call control. The bundle is source, not a deployment or permission grant.
4. Install the temporary `setupJarvisControls` backend function and invoke it only from an authenticated admin session. Its `initialize` action requires `confirm: INITIALIZE_JARVIS_CONTROLS`, creates only missing rows, never updates/deletes existing rows, reads before/after and fails closed on duplicates. It creates the two required singleton rows: (a) `owner_native_call` with `dispatch_state: idle` and `lock_until_ms: 0`; (b) `native_after_call_bridge` with `enabled: false`, `poll_lock_until_ms: 0`, and `native_naive_timestamps_utc: false`. Separate creates are not atomic; concurrent setup can still leave duplicates, which the function detects and blocks. Never create rows directly from the UI/client.
5. In the student HUD app’s server-side secrets, add `JARVIS_NATIVE_TOKEN` containing the student/Superagent-scoped token supplied through the approved Base44 credential flow. Never put it in prompts, frontend code, source control, logs or this bundle. If the token or scope is unavailable, stop with CALL ME disabled.
6. After manual UTC timestamp verification, invoke `setupJarvisControls` again with `action: enroll`, `confirm: ENROLL_JARVIS_AFTER_CALL_BRIDGE`, and `timestamp_policy_verified_utc: true`. It refuses enabled/already-enrolled bridges and any existing receipts; it sets the enrollment timestamp from the server clock and conditionally enables the bridge. This is not a history reset and does not reenroll blindly. **Remove/disable this temporary setup function after setup and record the test results.**
7. Review `workflow-template.json` in the student app’s current workflow UI. It is the exact sanitized DSL definition for an authenticated `afterCall` invocation every five minutes (`*/5 * * * *`, `Australia/Brisbane`), but is intentionally `status: draft` and `enabled: false`. Create no duplicate if an equivalent workflow exists. Enable only after the secret, both control rows, function auth and one controlled owner test are ready. Preserve existing workflows and do not use the legacy Automations API if the account’s current workflow UI offers the reviewed workflow mechanism. Confirm the function returns queued/202 and inspect `CallReceipt`; workflow completion alone is not execution proof.
7. Confirm the authenticated CALL ME button invokes `callMe`, the student’s control row is enabled only for the test, and `callStatus` reads only the student’s request. Make one owner-only test call; wait up to five minutes for pickup and 15 minutes total. Do not resend.
8. After the test, disable CALL ME if any auth, scope, transcript, duplicate, or execution check fails. Record exact errors and keep the student at partial/blocked readiness.

## Current-snapshot limitation

The generator now packages the exported current HUD entities/functions and reviewed native bridge runtime. It remains source plus manual instructions, not an installer: the facilitator must review schema merges, RLS, secret scope, workflow authentication and service-role bootstrap in the student app. Do not claim fresh-account proof or internal-route stability.



===== FILE: facilitator/setupJarvisControls/entry.ts =====
import { createClientFromRequest } from 'npm:@base44/sdk';
import { secrets } from 'base44:runtime';
import { handleSetup } from './setupJarvisControls.mjs';

export default async function(req: Request) {
  let base44;
  try { base44 = createClientFromRequest(req); } catch { return Response.json({ ok: false, error: 'unauthorized' }, { status: 401 }); }
  let token;
  try { token = secrets.get('JARVIS_NATIVE_TOKEN'); } catch { token = undefined; }
  return handleSetup(req, { base44, secretPresent: typeof token === 'string' && token.length > 0 });
}



===== FILE: facilitator/setupJarvisControls/setupJarvisControls.mjs =====
/* Temporary facilitator bootstrap core. Injects Base44 client, secret presence and clock for tests. */
const OWNER = 'owner_native_call';
const BRIDGE = 'native_after_call_bridge';
const json = (body, status = 200) => Response.json(body, { status, headers: { 'Cache-Control': 'no-store' } });

async function bodyOf(req) {
  const type = req.headers?.get?.('content-type') || '';
  if (!type.toLowerCase().startsWith('application/json')) return { error: 'json_content_type_required' };
  try {
    const raw = await req.text();
    if (new TextEncoder().encode(raw).byteLength > 4096) return { error: 'body_too_large' };
    const value = JSON.parse(raw);
    if (!value || typeof value !== 'object' || Array.isArray(value)) return { error: 'json_object_required' };
    return value;
  } catch { return { error: 'invalid_json' }; }
}

async function admin(base44) {
  try {
    const user = await base44.auth.me();
    return user?.role === 'admin';
  } catch { return false; }
}

async function rows(entity, query) { return entity.filter(query, '-created_date', 100); }
function exactOne(list) { return Array.isArray(list) && list.length === 1; }

export async function handleSetupUnsafe(req, { base44, secretPresent, now = () => Date.now() }) {
  if (req.method !== 'POST') return json({ ok: false, error: 'method_not_allowed' }, 405);
  // Authentication and secret presence precede body/action processing.
  if (!(await admin(base44))) return json({ ok: false, error: 'forbidden' }, 403);
  if (!secretPresent) return json({ ok: false, error: 'native_token_not_configured' }, 503);
  const body = await bodyOf(req);
  if (body.error) return json({ ok: false, error: body.error }, 400);
  const controls = base44.asServiceRole.entities.CallControl;
  const receipts = base44.asServiceRole.entities.CallReceipt;
  const action = body.action;
  if (action === 'initialize') {
    if (body.confirm !== 'INITIALIZE_JARVIS_CONTROLS') return json({ ok: false, error: 'explicit_confirmation_required' }, 400);
    const beforeOwner = await rows(controls, { name: OWNER });
    const beforeBridge = await rows(controls, { name: BRIDGE });
    if (beforeOwner.length > 1 || beforeBridge.length > 1) return json({ ok: false, error: 'duplicate_control_rows' }, 409);
    if (!beforeOwner.length) await controls.create({ name: OWNER, dispatch_state: 'idle', lock_until_ms: 0 });
    if (!beforeBridge.length) await controls.create({ name: BRIDGE, enabled: false, poll_lock_until_ms: 0, native_naive_timestamps_utc: false });
    // No update or delete is ever attempted. Separate creates are intentionally not claimed atomic.
    const afterOwner = await rows(controls, { name: OWNER });
    const afterBridge = await rows(controls, { name: BRIDGE });
    if (!exactOne(afterOwner) || !exactOne(afterBridge)) return json({ ok: false, error: 'control_bootstrap_not_unique' }, 409);
    return json({ ok: true, action, owner_native_call: 'present', native_after_call_bridge: 'present', warning: 'separate creates are not atomic; duplicate detection is fail-closed' });
  }
  if (action === 'enroll') {
    if (body.confirm !== 'ENROLL_JARVIS_AFTER_CALL_BRIDGE' || body.timestamp_policy_verified_utc !== true) return json({ ok: false, error: 'explicit_utc_confirmation_required' }, 400);
    const bridges = await rows(controls, { name: BRIDGE });
    const owners = await rows(controls, { name: OWNER });
    if (!exactOne(bridges) || !exactOne(owners)) return json({ ok: false, error: 'controls_not_unique' }, 409);
    const bridge = bridges[0];
    if (bridge.enabled === true || bridge.enrollment_at_ms != null) return json({ ok: false, error: 'already_enrolled' }, 409);
    const existingReceipts = await rows(receipts, {});
    if (existingReceipts.length) return json({ ok: false, error: 'existing_call_receipts_require_review' }, 409);
    const enrollment = now();
    if (!Number.isSafeInteger(enrollment) || enrollment <= 0) return json({ ok: false, error: 'invalid_server_clock' }, 500);
    const result = await controls.updateMany(
      { id: bridge.id, name: BRIDGE, enabled: false, enrollment_at_ms: bridge.enrollment_at_ms ?? null },
      { $set: { enabled: true, enrollment_at_ms: enrollment, native_naive_timestamps_utc: true } },
    );
    if (!result?.success || result.updated !== 1) return json({ ok: false, error: 'enrollment_not_applied' }, 409);
    const after = await rows(controls, { name: BRIDGE });
    if (!exactOne(after) || after[0].enabled !== true || after[0].enrollment_at_ms !== enrollment) return json({ ok: false, error: 'enrollment_readback_failed' }, 409);
    return json({ ok: true, action, enrolled_at_ms: enrollment, timestamp_policy: 'verified UTC naive timestamps' });
  }
  return json({ ok: false, error: 'unknown_action' }, 400);
}

export async function handleSetup(req, deps) {
  try { return await handleSetupUnsafe(req, deps); }
  catch { return json({ ok: false, error: 'setup_backend_failure' }, 503); }
}

===== FILE: source/AGENTS.md =====
# AGENTS.md

## Project Context

This is a Base44 app repository. Treat it as user-owned application code, keep changes focused on the user's request, and preserve existing project conventions.

Start with `README.md` for local setup, environment variables, and publish workflow.

## Base44 References

- CLI overview: https://docs.base44.com/developers/references/cli/get-started/overview.md
- Agent skills: https://docs.base44.com/developers/backend/overview/skills.md

If your agent supports Agent Skills, install or update Base44 skills before Base44-specific work:

```bash
npx skills add base44/skills
```

## Key Files

- `src/`: frontend application source.
- `src/api/base44Client.js`: frontend Base44 SDK client.
- `vite.config.js`: Vite config and Base44 Vite plugin setup.
- `.env.local`: local-only environment values; never commit secrets.

## Working Notes

- Use `base44 dev` as the default local development command when you need the local Base44 backend. It can run the backend and frontend together.
- When docs or code mention the frontend being started automatically, that usually means the Base44 project config includes `site.serveCommand`, for example `"serveCommand": "npm run dev"` in `base44/config.jsonc`.
- Use `npm run dev` only for frontend-only work against the hosted Base44 backend.
- Prefer the existing Base44 CLI workflow over adding new npm scripts for Base44-specific tasks.
- Reuse the existing SDK client and Vite plugin patterns before adding new Base44 integration paths.
- Run the relevant checks from `package.json` before finishing code changes.

===== FILE: source/README.md =====
# Base44 Project

Use this repository to run and edit the app locally, then publish changes back through Base44.

Any change pushed to the repo will also be reflected in the Base44 Builder.

## Prerequisites

1. Clone the repository using the project's Git URL.
2. Navigate to the project directory.
3. Install dependencies: `npm install`.
4. Install the Base44 CLI: `npm install -g base44@latest`.
5. Install [Deno](https://docs.deno.com/runtime/getting_started/installation/) — the local Base44 backend runs on it.

Run `base44 --help` (or see the [CLI reference](https://docs.base44.com/developers/references/cli/commands/introduction)) for the full command surface.

## Run Locally

Three commands, from the project root:

```bash
base44 login   # one-time per machine
base44 link    # one-time per clone
base44 dev     # local backend + frontend together
```

Open the frontend URL that `base44 dev` prints (typically `http://localhost:5173`).

Notes:

- **Every fresh clone needs `base44 link`.** It writes `base44/.app.jsonc` (the app-id pointer), which is deliberately gitignored. Your app id is in the Builder URL (`app.base44.com/apps/<id>/...`); `base44 link --help` shows the non-interactive flags.
- **`base44 dev` runs the frontend for you** (via `site.serveCommand` in this repo's `base44/config.jsonc`) — never run `npm run dev` yourself: alone it serves a UI with no backend behind it (`[base44] Proxy not enabled`, every `/api` call fails), and alongside `base44 dev` the second Vite silently takes the next port and you end up looking at the wrong one.
- **The app must be published at least once for the UI to load under `base44 dev`.** The frontend boots by fetching app settings from the hosted app; before the first publish that fails and every page redirects to login. The local API works regardless.
- Entities, functions, and auth run locally — entity data is **in-memory only**, wiped when `base44 dev` restarts. Everything else (Core integrations, OAuth login) is forwarded to your deployed app. Full breakdown: [Local development overview](https://docs.base44.com/developers/backend/overview/local-dev/local-development-overview).

## Frontend Only, Hosted Backend

To work on just the frontend against your app's live hosted backend:

```bash
base44 dev --remote
```

⚠️ In this mode writes go to your app's **production data** — plain `base44 dev` keeps everything local.

## Publish Your Changes

After pushing your changes to git, open the Base44 dashboard and publish the app:

```bash
base44 dashboard open
```

This repo syncs to Base44 through git, so publish from the dashboard rather than `base44 deploy` — a CLI deploy ships your local tree directly, bypassing the sync, and the deployed state silently diverges from the repo.

## Docs & Support

GitHub integration: [https://docs.base44.com/developers/app-code/local-development/github](https://docs.base44.com/developers/app-code/local-development/github)

Local development: [https://docs.base44.com/developers/backend/overview/local-dev/local-development-overview](https://docs.base44.com/developers/backend/overview/local-dev/local-development-overview)

Support: [https://app.base44.com/support](https://app.base44.com/support)

===== FILE: source/base44/functions/afterCall/entry.ts =====
import { createClientFromRequest } from 'npm:@base44/sdk';
import { secrets, waitUntil } from 'base44:runtime';
import { runAfterCallPoll } from '../../shared/after-call-bridge.ts';
export default async function(req) {
  if(req.method !== 'POST') return Response.json({error:'method_not_allowed'},{status:405});
  const b=createClientFromRequest(req);
  let user;
  try { user=await b.auth.me(); } catch { return Response.json({error:'unauthorized'},{status:401}); }
  if(user?.role!=='admin') return Response.json({error:'forbidden'},{status:403});
  const pat=secrets.get('JARVIS_NATIVE_TOKEN');
  if(!pat) return Response.json({error:'native_token_not_configured'},{status:503});
  const run=async()=>{
    try {
      const result=await runAfterCallPoll({store:{control:b.asServiceRole.entities.CallControl,receipt:b.asServiceRole.entities.CallReceipt},pat});
      if(result.processed || !['ok','poll_lock_busy','not_configured'].includes(result.reason)) {
        await b.asServiceRole.entities.ActivityLog.create({type:'note',task_title:'Jarvis after-call bridge',message:'After-call bridge: '+JSON.stringify(result)});
      }
    } catch {
      await b.asServiceRole.entities.ActivityLog.create({type:'note',task_title:'Jarvis after-call bridge',message:'After-call bridge failed; check receipt status before retrying.'}).catch(()=>{});
    }
  };
  waitUntil(new Promise(resolve=>setTimeout(()=>resolve(run()),0)));
  return Response.json({ok:true,queued:true},{status:202,headers:{'Cache-Control':'no-store'}});
}



===== FILE: source/base44/functions/callMe/entry.ts =====
import { createClientFromRequest } from 'npm:@base44/sdk';
import { secrets, waitUntil } from 'base44:runtime';
import { postWithDeliveryConfirmation } from '../../shared/after-call-bridge.ts';

const AGENT_ID = '__STUDENT_SUPERAGENT_ID__';
const BASE = 'https://app.base44.com/api/apps/' + AGENT_ID + '/user-agent/conversations';
const CONTROL_NAME = 'owner_native_call';
const COOLDOWN_MS = 60_000;
const UNKNOWN_HOLD_MS = 600_000;
const REVISION = 'native-report-first-2026-09-24-preferences';
const PROMPT =
  'Call only the registered owner once using the native make_phone_call tool. ' +
  'Do not call another person or automatically redial. First read the latest HUD context ' +
  'using the native authenticated backend-function tool: getContext for HUD app ' +
  '__STUDENT_HUD_APP_ID__ with empty payload. Its verified admin-session access is supported. ' +
  'Never print credentials or present old chat snapshots as current. ' +
  'Put the following conversation rules explicitly in the native phone tool purpose/goal, ' +
  'with only the concise verified facts needed for this call, not a backlog dump: ' +
  'Your first words are "Hey __STUDENT_OWNER_NAME__, it\'s Jarvis." Give ONE brief factual report of work ' +
  'actually completed today in Australia/Brisbane and any blocker needing the owner. ' +
  'Immediately after the greeting, SAY the actual report in two or three short sentences. ' +
  'Do not merely announce that you have an update, ask permission to give it, or jump ' +
  'to the closing question before delivering the report. Include at most one useful ' +
  'idea if it follows from the verified facts, clearly labeled as a suggestion. ' +
  'Profile.call_hours is a preferred outreach window, not a prohibition on working or ' +
  'calling at other appropriate times. Do not tell the owner to wait until tomorrow ' +
  'merely because that preferred window has passed. Never invent an availability rule. ' +
  'Do not describe old work as today\'s work; if nothing is verified, say so briefly. ' +
  'Then ask "Is there anything else you\'d like me to do or talk through?" ' +
  'The report is only the opening, never a recurring agenda. From then on, follow the ' +
  'owner\'s current conversation naturally: answer questions, discuss ideas, and take requests. ' +
  'If interrupted, yield and follow the new topic immediately. Do not resume or repeat ' +
  'the report or bring back old tasks unless the owner explicitly asks for the report ' +
  'again or for clarification about it. Ordinary conversation does not authorize tasks. ' +
  'Keep spoken turns short and ask only one necessary clarifying question at a time. ' +
  'For an actual task request, confirm unclear names, locations, counts or deadlines; ' +
  'the owner\'s latest correction replaces the earlier version. Do not start lengthy ' +
  'research during the call or claim unverified live results or board writes. Explain ' +
  'that agreed research and execution happen AFTER hang-up. Do not end the call because ' +
  'the report or one request is finished. When the owner indicates they are ready to ' +
  'finish, briefly confirm any agreed actions, let them finish or correct you, then say ' +
  'goodbye. If they continue speaking, continue the conversation instead of hanging up. ' +
  'End of spoken-call rules. The following is post-call routing, not a spoken briefing: ' +
  'After hang-up the HUD afterCall background bridge checks every five minutes and sends ' +
  'the exact call ID to a separate tool-enabled conversation for CALL WRAP-UP AND EXECUTION v4. ' +
  'That worker captures and executes safe authorized tasks and saves verified HUD results. ' +
  'The automatic [Outbound call] ended reply in THIS conversation has no tools; give a ' +
  'truthful acknowledgment and explain that transcript processing can take up to five minutes ' +
  'to begin. Do not describe that tool-less notification as a failure of the execution bridge, ' +
  'and do not claim the task is already complete. Only the actual owner transcript authorizes ' +
  'tasks, not old project chores recited by the phone assistant. ' +
  'Respect all approval boundaries. ' +
  'Do not claim ringing until the native tool confirms dialing or claim execution without evidence.';

function json(body, status) {
  return Response.json(body, {
    status,
    headers: { 'Cache-Control': 'no-store', ...(status === 405 ? { Allow: 'POST' } : {}) },
  });
}

function post(url, token, body, timeoutMs = 15_000) {
  return fetch(url, {
    method: 'POST',
    redirect: 'manual', // Edge runtime supports manual, not error; never follow redirects.
    signal: AbortSignal.timeout(timeoutMs),
    headers: { Authorization: 'Bearer ' + token, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
    body: JSON.stringify(body),
  });
}

async function dispatchQueued({ controls, token, controlId, requestId, logs }) {
  const recordFailure = async (reason) => {
    try { await logs.create({type:'note',task_title:'Jarvis call dispatch',message:'Call request '+requestId+' failed before dialing: '+reason}); } catch {}
  };
  const updateOwned = async (data) => {
    const result = await controls.updateMany({ id: controlId, request_id: requestId }, { $set: data });
    if (!result.success || result.updated !== 1) throw new Error('control_write_failed');
  };
  let conversationId;
  try {
    const response = await post(BASE + '/v2', token, { metadata: {} });
    if (!response.ok) {
      await recordFailure('native conversation HTTP '+response.status);
      await updateOwned({ dispatch_state: [401, 403].includes(response.status) ? 'failed' : 'failed' });
      return;
    }
    const data = await response.json();
    if (typeof data.id !== 'string' || !/^[a-f0-9]{24}$/.test(data.id)) throw new Error('invalid_conversation');
    conversationId = data.id;
  } catch (e) {
    await recordFailure(String(e?.name)+': '+String(e?.message).replaceAll(String(token),'[REDACTED]').slice(0,250));
    try { await updateOwned({ dispatch_state: 'failed' }); } catch {}
    return;
  }
  const messageId = crypto.randomUUID();
  try {
    await updateOwned({ dispatch_state: 'submitting', conversation_id: conversationId, message_id: messageId, lock_until_ms: Date.now() + UNKNOWN_HOLD_MS });
    const delivery = await postWithDeliveryConfirmation(fetch, BASE + '/' + conversationId + '/messages', token, {
      id: messageId, role: 'user', hidden: false, content: PROMPT,
    }, conversationId, messageId);
    if (delivery.kind === 'unknown') {
      await updateOwned({ dispatch_state: 'unknown', lock_until_ms: Date.now() + UNKNOWN_HOLD_MS });
      return;
    }
    if (delivery.kind === 'rejected') {
      await updateOwned({ dispatch_state: 'failed', lock_until_ms: Date.now() + COOLDOWN_MS });
      return;
    }
    await updateOwned({ dispatch_state: 'submitted', lock_until_ms: Date.now() + COOLDOWN_MS });
  } catch {
    try { await updateOwned({ dispatch_state: 'unknown', lock_until_ms: Date.now() + UNKNOWN_HOLD_MS }); } catch {}
  }
}

export default async function(req) {
  if (req.method !== 'POST') return json({ requestAccepted: false, error: 'method_not_allowed', revision: REVISION }, 405);
  let base44;
  let user;
  try {
    base44 = createClientFromRequest(req);
    user = await base44.auth.me();
  } catch {
    return json({ requestAccepted: false, error: 'unauthorized' }, 401);
  }
  if (!user) return json({ requestAccepted: false, error: 'unauthorized' }, 401);
  if (user.role !== 'admin') return json({ requestAccepted: false, error: 'forbidden' }, 403);

  // Admin-only diagnostic mode follows the real auth/config gates but can never
  // claim a dispatch, queue work, create a conversation or dial. Never coerce it.
  let input;
  try {
    const raw = await req.text();
    if (new TextEncoder().encode(raw).length > 4096) return json({ error: 'body_too_large' }, 413);
    input = raw.trim() ? JSON.parse(raw) : {};
    if (!input || typeof input !== 'object' || Array.isArray(input) || ('dry_run' in input && typeof input.dry_run !== 'boolean')) return json({ error: 'invalid_body' }, 400);
  } catch { return json({ error: 'invalid_json' }, 400); }

  // The client cannot select the token, agent, URL, phone number, or prompt.
  const token = secrets.get('JARVIS_NATIVE_TOKEN');
  if (!token) return json({ requestAccepted: false, error: 'native_token_not_configured' }, 503);

  try {
    const controls = base44.asServiceRole.entities.CallControl;
    const rows = await controls.filter({ name: CONTROL_NAME }, '-created_date', 2);
    if (rows.length !== 1) {
      return json({ requestAccepted: false, error: rows.length ? 'control_not_unique' : 'control_not_initialized' }, 503);
    }
    const control = rows[0];
    if (input.dry_run === true) {
      const blocked = ['submitting', 'unknown'].includes(control.dispatch_state);
      const cooling = !Number.isFinite(control.lock_until_ms) || control.lock_until_ms > Date.now();
      const diagnostic = { dry_run: true, dialed: false, requestAccepted: false, auth_verified: true,
        native_token_configured: true, control_ready: true, would_queue: !blocked && !cooling,
        reason: blocked ? 'dispatch_unknown' : cooling ? 'cooldown_active' : 'ready', revision: REVISION };
      // Optional audit row for a scheduled no-dial test. Contains no credentials,
      // phone numbers, or caller-controlled text. Does not change call control.
      if (input.audit_dry_run === true) await base44.asServiceRole.entities.ActivityLog.create({
        type: 'note', task_title: 'EOD NO-DIAL DIAGNOSTIC', message: JSON.stringify(diagnostic),
      });
      return json(diagnostic, 200);
    }
    // These states require reconciliation, even after a cooldown expires.
    if (['submitting', 'unknown'].includes(control.dispatch_state)) {
      return json({ requestAccepted: false, error: 'dispatch_unknown' }, 409);
    }
    const requestId = crypto.randomUUID();
    const now = Date.now();
    const claim = await controls.updateMany(
      { id: control.id, lock_until_ms: { $lte: now }, dispatch_state: { $nin: ['submitting', 'unknown'] } },
      { $set: { request_id: requestId, lock_until_ms: now + COOLDOWN_MS, dispatch_state: 'claimed', conversation_id: '', message_id: '' } },
    );
    if (!claim.success || claim.updated !== 1) return json({ requestAccepted: false, error: 'cooldown_active' }, 429);

    // Defer invocation one turn so the 202 response is committed before any upstream I/O.
    waitUntil(new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(() => resolve(dispatchQueued({ controls, token, controlId: control.id, requestId, logs:base44.asServiceRole.entities.ActivityLog })), 0)));
    return json({ requestAccepted: true, request_id: requestId, state: 'queued' }, 202);
  } catch {
    return json({ requestAccepted: false, error: 'call_control_unavailable' }, 503);
  }
}



===== FILE: source/base44/functions/callStatus/entry.ts =====
import { createClientFromRequest } from 'npm:@base44/sdk';

const CONTROL_NAME = 'owner_native_call';
const UUID = /^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{12}$/i;

function json(body: Record<string, unknown>, status = 200) {
  return Response.json(body, { status, headers: { 'Cache-Control': 'no-store' } });
}

export default async function(req: Request) {
  if (req.method !== 'POST') return json({ ok: false, error: 'method_not_allowed' }, 405);
  let base44: any, user: any, body: any;
  try {
    base44 = createClientFromRequest(req);
    user = await base44.auth.me();
  } catch { return json({ ok: false, error: 'unauthorized' }, 401); }
  if (!user) return json({ ok: false, error: 'unauthorized' }, 401);
  if (user.role !== 'admin') return json({ ok: false, error: 'forbidden' }, 403);
  try { body = await req.json(); } catch { return json({ ok: false, error: 'invalid_json' }, 400); }
  if (!UUID.test(body?.request_id || '')) return json({ ok: false, error: 'invalid_request_id' }, 400);
  const rows = await base44.asServiceRole.entities.CallControl.filter({ name: CONTROL_NAME, request_id: body.request_id }, '-updated_date', 2);
  if (rows.length !== 1) return json({ ok: false, error: 'request_not_found' }, 404);
  const row = rows[0];
  return json({ ok: true, request_id: body.request_id, state: row.dispatch_state || 'unknown', ...(typeof row.conversation_id === 'string' && row.conversation_id ? { conversation_id: row.conversation_id } : {}) });
}

===== FILE: source/base44/functions/getContext/entry.ts =====
import { method405, authorizeWrite } from '../../shared/hud.ts';

function txt(res, status) {
  return new Response(res, { status, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'text/plain; charset=utf-8' } });
}

export default async function(req) {
  try {
    const m405 = method405(req);
    if (m405) return m405;

    const auth = await authorizeWrite(req);
    if (!auth.ok) return txt("unauthorized", 401);
    const base44 = auth.base44;

    // Backwards-compatible optional leads-only paginated view for native review.
    let body = {};
    try { const raw = await req.text(); if (raw) body = JSON.parse(raw); } catch {}
    if (body && body.section === 'leads') {
      let limit = 100;
      let offset = 0;
      if (body.limit !== undefined) {
        if (!Number.isInteger(body.limit) || body.limit < 1) return Response.json({ error: 'limit must be a positive integer' }, { status: 400, headers: { 'Cache-Control': 'no-store' } });
        limit = Math.min(body.limit, 500);
      }
      if (body.offset !== undefined) {
        if (!Number.isInteger(body.offset) || body.offset < 0) return Response.json({ error: 'offset must be a non-negative integer' }, { status: 400, headers: { 'Cache-Control': 'no-store' } });
        offset = body.offset;
      }
      const all = await base44.asServiceRole.entities.Lead.list();
      all.sort((a, b) => {
        const d = new Date(b.created_date || 0).getTime() - new Date(a.created_date || 0).getTime();
        if (d !== 0) return d;
        return String(a.id || '').localeCompare(String(b.id || ''));
      });
      const total_count = all.length;
      const page = all.slice(offset, offset + limit);
      const has_more = offset + page.length < total_count;
      const leads = page.map((l) => ({
        id: l.id, business_name: l.business_name, trade: l.trade, suburb: l.suburb,
        phone: l.phone, website: l.website, google_rating: l.google_rating,
        review_count: l.review_count, source_url: l.source_url, status: l.status, notes: l.notes
      }));
      return Response.json({ section: 'leads', total_count, offset, limit, has_more, leads }, { status: 200, headers: { 'Cache-Control': 'no-store' } });
    }

    const lines = [];

    // 1. PROFILE
    const profiles = await base44.asServiceRole.entities.Profile.list();
    lines.push("PROFILE");
    const profile = profiles[0];
    if (profile) {
      const fields = [
        ['business_name', profile.business_name],
        ['owner_name', profile.owner_name],
        ['niche', profile.niche],
        ['service', profile.service],
        ['suburbs', profile.suburbs],
        ['price_low', profile.price_low],
        ['price_high', profile.price_high],
        ['tools', profile.tools],
        ['call_hours', profile.call_hours],
        ['timezone', profile.timezone],
        ['checkin_time', profile.checkin_time],
        ['goal_90_day', profile.goal_90_day],
        ['updated_at', profile.updated_at]
      ];
      for (const [label, value] of fields) {
        if (value !== undefined && value !== null && value !== '') {
          lines.push(`${label}: ${value}`);
        }
      }
    } else {
      lines.push("(no profile)");
    }
    lines.push("");

    // 2. MEMORY (20 newest)
    lines.push("MEMORY");
    const memories = await base44.asServiceRole.entities.Memory.list('-created_date', 20);
    if (memories.length === 0) {
      lines.push("(none)");
    } else {
      for (const m of memories) {
        lines.push(m.text || '');
      }
    }
    lines.push("");

    // 3. OPEN SESSIONS
    lines.push("OPEN SESSIONS");
    const sessions = await base44.asServiceRole.entities.SessionLog.filter({ status: 'open' });
    if (sessions.length === 0) {
      lines.push("(none)");
    } else {
      for (const s of sessions) {
        lines.push(`${s.title || ''} — ${s.job || ''}`);
      }
    }
    lines.push("");

    // 4. OPEN TASKS
    lines.push("OPEN TASKS");
    const tasks = await base44.asServiceRole.entities.Task.list();
    const openTasks = tasks.filter((t) => t.status !== 'done');
    if (openTasks.length === 0) {
      lines.push("(none)");
    } else {
      for (const t of openTasks) {
        lines.push(`${t.title || ''} | status=${t.status || ''} | priority=${t.priority || ''} | due=${t.due_date || 'none'}`);
      }
    }
    lines.push("");

    // 5. LEADS
    lines.push("LEADS");
    const leads = await base44.asServiceRole.entities.Lead.list();
    const counts = {};
    for (const l of leads) {
      const st = l.status || 'new';
      counts[st] = (counts[st] || 0) + 1;
    }
    const statusOrder = ['new', 'demo_built', 'contacted', 'replied', 'won', 'lost'];
    let printedAny = false;
    for (const st of statusOrder) {
      if (counts[st]) {
        lines.push(`${st}: ${counts[st]}`);
        printedAny = true;
      }
    }
    if (!printedAny) lines.push("(none)");
    lines.push("");
    const newestLeads = [...leads]
      .sort((a, b) => new Date(b.created_date) - new Date(a.created_date))
      .slice(0, 5);
    for (const l of newestLeads) {
      lines.push(`${l.business_name || ''} — ${l.trade || ''} — ${l.suburb || ''} — ${l.status || ''}`);
    }

    return txt(lines.join("\n"), 200);
  } catch (error) {
    return txt("error", 400);
  }
}

===== FILE: source/base44/functions/logActivity/entry.ts =====
import { method405, readBody, authorizeWrite } from '../../shared/hud.ts';

function txt(res, status) {
  return new Response(res, { status, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'text/plain; charset=utf-8' } });
}

export default async function(req) {
  try {
    const m405 = method405(req);
    if (m405) return m405;

    const auth = await authorizeWrite(req);
    if (!auth.ok) return txt("unauthorized", 401);
    const base44 = auth.base44;

    const parsed = await readBody(req);
    if (!parsed.ok) return Response.json({ error: parsed.error }, { status: parsed.status });
    const body = parsed.body;

    const message = typeof body?.message === 'string' ? body.message : null;
    const type = typeof body?.type === 'string' ? body.type : null;
    const taskTitle = typeof body?.task_title === 'string' ? body.task_title : undefined;

    if (!message || !type) {
      return Response.json({ error: 'message and type are required strings' }, { status: 400 });
    }

    const record = await base44.asServiceRole.entities.ActivityLog.create({
      message,
      type,
      task_title: taskTitle
    });

    return Response.json({ ok: true, id: record.id });
  } catch (error) {
    return Response.json({ error: 'bad request' }, { status: 400 });
  }
}

===== FILE: source/base44/functions/logSession/entry.ts =====
import { method405, readBody, validateEnum, todayISO, authorizeWrite } from '../../shared/hud.ts';

function txt(res, status) {
  return new Response(res, { status, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'text/plain; charset=utf-8' } });
}

const SESSION_STATUS = ['open', 'done'];

export default async function(req) {
  try {
    const m405 = method405(req);
    if (m405) return m405;

    const auth = await authorizeWrite(req);
    if (!auth.ok) return txt("unauthorized", 401);

    const parsed = await readBody(req);
    if (!parsed.ok) return txt(parsed.error, parsed.status);
    const body = parsed.body;

    const title = typeof body?.title === 'string' ? body.title.trim() : null;
    if (!title) {
      return txt("title is required", 400);
    }
    const job = typeof body?.job === 'string' ? body.job : '';
    let status = 'open';
    if (typeof body?.status === 'string') {
      const v = validateEnum(body.status, SESSION_STATUS, 'status');
      if (!v.ok) return txt(v.error, 400);
      status = body.status;
    }
    const outcome = typeof body?.outcome === 'string' ? body.outcome : undefined;
    const conversationRef = typeof body?.conversation_ref === 'string' ? body.conversation_ref : undefined;

    const base44 = auth.base44;
    const SessionLog = base44.asServiceRole.entities.SessionLog;
    const existing = await SessionLog.filter({ title });

    if (existing.length === 0) {
      if (status === 'done') {
        return Response.json({ error: `no open session called ${title}` }, { status: 404 });
      }
      await SessionLog.create({
        title,
        job,
        status,
        conversation_ref: conversationRef,
        started_at: todayISO()
      });
      return txt(`session logged: ${title} (${status})`, 200);
    }

    const session = existing[0];
    if (status === 'done') {
      const update = { status: 'done', ended_at: todayISO() };
      if (outcome !== undefined) update.outcome = outcome;
      if (job) update.job = job;
      if (conversationRef !== undefined) update.conversation_ref = conversationRef;
      await SessionLog.update(session.id, update);
      return txt(`session logged: ${title} (done)`, 200);
    }

    const update = {};
    if (job) update.job = job;
    if (conversationRef !== undefined) update.conversation_ref = conversationRef;
    if (Object.keys(update).length > 0) {
      await SessionLog.update(session.id, update);
    }
    return txt(`session logged: ${title} (open)`, 200);
  } catch (error) {
    return txt("bad request", 400);
  }
}

===== FILE: source/base44/functions/remember/entry.ts =====
import { method405, readBody, validateEnum, todayISO, authorizeWrite } from '../../shared/hud.ts';

function txt(res, status) {
  return new Response(res, { status, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'text/plain; charset=utf-8' } });
}

const VALID_KINDS = ['fact', 'decision', 'event', 'question', 'outcome'];

export default async function(req) {
  try {
    const m405 = method405(req);
    if (m405) return m405;

    const auth = await authorizeWrite(req);
    if (!auth.ok) return txt("unauthorized", 401);
    const base44 = auth.base44;

    const parsed = await readBody(req);
    if (!parsed.ok) return txt(parsed.error, parsed.status);
    const body = parsed.body;

    const text = typeof body?.text === 'string' ? body.text : null;
    if (!text || !text.trim()) {
      return txt("text is required", 400);
    }
    let kind = 'event';
    if (typeof body?.kind === 'string') {
      const v = validateEnum(body.kind, VALID_KINDS, 'kind');
      if (!v.ok) return txt(v.error, 400);
      kind = body.kind;
    }
    const subject = typeof body?.subject === 'string' ? body.subject : undefined;
    const source = typeof body?.source === 'string' ? body.source : undefined;
    const today = todayISO();

    const Memory = base44.asServiceRole.entities.Memory;
    const existing = await Memory.filter({ text });
    const match = existing.find((m) => m.created_at === today);

    const data = { text, kind, subject, source, created_at: today };
    if (match) {
      await Memory.update(match.id, data);
    } else {
      await Memory.create(data);
    }

    return txt(`remembered: ${text}`, 200);
  } catch (error) {
    return txt("bad request", 400);
  }
}

===== FILE: source/base44/functions/upsertLead/entry.ts =====
import { method405, readBody, leadKey, validateEnum, findMatches, dedupeMatches, todayISO, normalizeDate, authorizeWrite, brisbaneTodayISO } from '../../shared/hud.ts';

function txt(res, status) {
  return new Response(res, { status, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'text/plain; charset=utf-8' } });
}

const LEAD_STATUS = ['new', 'demo_built', 'contacted', 'replied', 'won', 'lost'];

function normalizedString(body, canonical, alias) {
  const hasCanonical = Object.hasOwn(body, canonical);
  const hasAlias = Object.hasOwn(body, alias);
  if (hasCanonical && typeof body[canonical] !== 'string') throw new Error(`${canonical} must be text`);
  if (hasAlias && typeof body[alias] !== 'string') throw new Error(`${alias} must be text`);
  return hasCanonical ? body[canonical] : hasAlias ? body[alias] : undefined;
}

export default async function(req) {
  try {
    const m405 = method405(req);
    if (m405) return m405;

    const auth = await authorizeWrite(req);
    if (!auth.ok) return txt("unauthorized", 401);
    const base44 = auth.base44;

    const parsed = await readBody(req);
    if (!parsed.ok) return txt(parsed.error, parsed.status);
    const body = parsed.body;

    const businessNameValue = normalizedString(body, 'business_name', 'name');
    const tradeValue = normalizedString(body, 'trade', 'business_type');
    const sourceUrlValue = normalizedString(body, 'source_url', 'source');
    const businessName = typeof businessNameValue === 'string' ? businessNameValue.trim() : null;
    if (!businessName) {
      return txt("business_name is required", 400);
    }
    const suburbRaw = typeof body?.suburb === 'string' ? body.suburb.trim() : '';
    if (!suburbRaw) {
      return txt("business_name and suburb are required", 400);
    }

    if (typeof body?.status === 'string') {
      const v = validateEnum(body.status, LEAD_STATUS, 'status');
      if (!v.ok) return txt(v.error, 400);
    }

    let wonAtISO;
    if (typeof body?.won_at === 'string' && body.won_at.trim()) {
      const d = normalizeDate(body.won_at);
      if (!d.ok) return txt(d.error, 400);
      wonAtISO = d.iso;
    }
    const dealValue = typeof body?.deal_value === 'number' ? body.deal_value : undefined;

    const Lead = base44.asServiceRole.entities.Lead;
    const key = leadKey(businessName, suburbRaw);
    const matches = await findMatches(Lead, (r) => leadKey(r.business_name, r.suburb), key);

    if (matches.length > 0) {
      const existing = matches[0];
      const update = {};
      if (tradeValue !== undefined) update.trade = tradeValue;
      if (typeof body?.suburb === 'string') update.suburb = body.suburb;
      if (typeof body?.phone === 'string') update.phone = body.phone;
      if (typeof body?.website === 'string') update.website = body.website;
      if (typeof body?.google_rating === 'number') update.google_rating = body.google_rating;
      if (typeof body?.review_count === 'number') update.review_count = body.review_count;
      if (sourceUrlValue !== undefined) update.source_url = sourceUrlValue;
      if (typeof body?.status === 'string') update.status = body.status;
      if (typeof body?.demo_url === 'string') update.demo_url = body.demo_url;
      if (typeof body?.pitch === 'string') update.pitch = body.pitch;
      if (typeof body?.notes === 'string') update.notes = body.notes;
      if (dealValue !== undefined) update.deal_value = dealValue;
      // won_at: on transition to won, set Brisbane today if not provided and not already set.
      const newStatus = typeof body?.status === 'string' ? body.status : existing.status;
      if (newStatus === 'won') {
        if (wonAtISO !== undefined) {
          update.won_at = wonAtISO;
        } else if (existing.status !== 'won' || !existing.won_at) {
          update.won_at = brisbaneTodayISO();
        }
      } else if (wonAtISO !== undefined) {
        update.won_at = wonAtISO;
      }
      await Lead.update(existing.id, update);
      await dedupeMatches(Lead, matches);
      const finalStatus = update.status || existing.status || 'new';
      return txt(`lead updated: ${businessName} (${finalStatus})`, 200);
    }

    const trade = typeof tradeValue === 'string' ? tradeValue : '';
    const phone = typeof body?.phone === 'string' ? body.phone : undefined;
    const website = typeof body?.website === 'string' ? body.website : undefined;
    const googleRating = typeof body?.google_rating === 'number' ? body.google_rating : undefined;
    const reviewCount = typeof body?.review_count === 'number' ? body.review_count : undefined;
    const sourceUrl = typeof sourceUrlValue === 'string' ? sourceUrlValue : '';
    const status = typeof body?.status === 'string' ? body.status : 'new';
    const demoUrl = typeof body?.demo_url === 'string' ? body.demo_url : undefined;
    const pitch = typeof body?.pitch === 'string' ? body.pitch : undefined;
    const notes = typeof body?.notes === 'string' ? body.notes : undefined;
    const finalWonAt = status === 'won'
      ? (wonAtISO !== undefined ? wonAtISO : brisbaneTodayISO())
      : (wonAtISO !== undefined ? wonAtISO : undefined);

    const data = {
      business_name: businessName,
      trade,
      suburb: suburbRaw,
      phone,
      website,
      google_rating: googleRating,
      review_count: reviewCount,
      source_url: sourceUrl,
      status,
      demo_url: demoUrl,
      pitch,
      notes,
      deal_value: dealValue,
      won_at: finalWonAt
    };

    await Lead.create(data);
    const rematches = await findMatches(Lead, (r) => leadKey(r.business_name, r.suburb), key);
    await dedupeMatches(Lead, rematches);

    try {
      await base44.asServiceRole.entities.Memory.create({
        text: `New lead found: ${businessName} — ${trade} — ${suburbRaw}`,
        kind: 'event',
        created_at: todayISO()
      });
    } catch (e) {}

    return txt(`lead created: ${businessName} (${status})`, 200);
  } catch (error) {
    return txt("bad request", 400);
  }
}


===== FILE: source/base44/functions/upsertTask/entry.ts =====
import { method405, readBody, taskKey, normalizeDate, validateEnum, findMatches, dedupeMatches, authorizeWrite, brisbaneTodayISO } from '../../shared/hud.ts';

function txt(res, status) {
  return new Response(res, { status, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'text/plain; charset=utf-8' } });
}

const TASK_STATUS = ['todo', 'doing', 'done'];
const PRIORITY = ['low', 'normal', 'high'];

export default async function(req) {
  try {
    const m405 = method405(req);
    if (m405) return m405;

    const auth = await authorizeWrite(req);
    if (!auth.ok) return txt("unauthorized", 401);
    const base44 = auth.base44;

    const parsed = await readBody(req);
    if (!parsed.ok) return Response.json({ error: parsed.error }, { status: parsed.status });
    const body = parsed.body;

    const title = typeof body?.title === 'string' ? body.title.trim() : null;
    if (!title) {
      return Response.json({ error: 'title is required' }, { status: 400 });
    }
    if (Object.hasOwn(body, 'execution_owner') && !['jarvis', 'owner'].includes(body.execution_owner)) {
      return Response.json({ error: 'invalid execution_owner' }, { status: 400 });
    }
    if (Object.hasOwn(body, 'progress_note') && (typeof body.progress_note !== 'string' || body.progress_note.length > 1000)) {
      return Response.json({ error: 'progress_note must be text up to 1000 characters' }, { status: 400 });
    }
    // Only the server may timestamp a progress report. Never trust browser/model time.
    if (Object.hasOwn(body, 'progress_updated_at')) {
      return Response.json({ error: 'progress_updated_at is server-managed' }, { status: 400 });
    }

    let status = 'todo';
    if (typeof body?.status === 'string') {
      const v = validateEnum(body.status, TASK_STATUS, 'status');
      if (!v.ok) return Response.json({ error: v.error }, { status: 400 });
      status = body.status;
    }
    if (typeof body?.priority === 'string') {
      const v = validateEnum(body.priority, PRIORITY, 'priority');
      if (!v.ok) return Response.json({ error: v.error }, { status: 400 });
    }
    let dueISO;
    if (typeof body?.due_date === 'string') {
      const d = normalizeDate(body.due_date);
      if (!d.ok) return Response.json({ error: d.error }, { status: 400 });
      dueISO = d.iso;
    }

    const Task = base44.asServiceRole.entities.Task;
    const key = taskKey(title);
    const matches = await findMatches(Task, (r) => taskKey(r.title), key);
    const existing = matches[0];
    // Partial notes/progress updates must not reopen a task or clear done_at.
    const effectiveStatus = typeof body.status === 'string' ? body.status : (existing?.status || 'todo');
    const transitioned = existing ? effectiveStatus !== existing.status : true;
    const progress = {};
    if (typeof body.execution_owner === 'string') progress.execution_owner = body.execution_owner;
    if (typeof body.progress_note === 'string') progress.progress_note = body.progress_note;
    if (transitioned || Object.hasOwn(body, 'progress_note') || Object.hasOwn(body, 'execution_owner')) {
      progress.progress_updated_at = new Date().toISOString();
    }

    const brisToday = brisbaneTodayISO();
    const memToday = new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10);

    let created = false;
    if (matches.length > 0) {
      const update = { ...progress };
      if (typeof body?.status === 'string') update.status = body.status;
      if (typeof body?.priority === 'string') update.priority = body.priority;
      if (dueISO !== undefined) update.due_date = dueISO;
      if (typeof body?.notes === 'string') update.notes = body.notes;
      // done_at: set on a real transition to done, clear on reopening.
      if (effectiveStatus === 'done') {
        if (existing.status !== 'done' || !existing.done_at) update.done_at = brisToday;
      } else {
        if (existing.done_at) update.done_at = null;
      }
      await Task.update(existing.id, update);
      await dedupeMatches(Task, matches);
    } else {
      const priority = typeof body?.priority === 'string' ? body.priority : 'normal';
      const notes = typeof body?.notes === 'string' ? body.notes : undefined;
      const doneAt = effectiveStatus === 'done' ? brisToday : undefined;
      await Task.create({ title, status: effectiveStatus, priority, due_date: dueISO, notes, done_at: doneAt, ...progress });
      created = true;
      const rematches = await findMatches(Task, (r) => taskKey(r.title), key);
      await dedupeMatches(Task, rematches);
    }

    const logType = transitioned && effectiveStatus === 'doing' ? 'task_started' : transitioned && effectiveStatus === 'done' ? 'task_done' : 'note';
    const message =
      logType === 'task_started' ? `Started: ${title}` :
      logType === 'task_done' ? `Completed: ${title}` :
      `Updated: ${title}`;
    try {
      await base44.asServiceRole.entities.ActivityLog.create({
        message, type: logType, task_title: title
      });
    } catch (e) {}
    if (effectiveStatus === 'done' && transitioned) {
      try {
        await base44.asServiceRole.entities.Memory.create({
          text: `Finished task: ${title}`, kind: 'outcome', created_at: memToday
        });
      } catch (e) {}
    }

    return txt(created ? `task created: ${title}` : `task updated: ${title}`, 200);
  } catch (error) {
    return Response.json({ error: 'bad request' }, { status: 400 });
  }
}

===== FILE: source/base44/shared/after-call-bridge.ts =====
/* After-call poller library; importing the module performs no remote side effects.
 * The store methods mirror Base44 entity methods; updateMany must be an atomic conditional
 * update and the caller must verify updated === 1.
 */

export const BREV_APP_ID = '__STUDENT_SUPERAGENT_ID__';
export const BASE = `https://app.base44.com/api/apps/${BREV_APP_ID}`;
export const CONTROL_NAME = 'native_after_call_bridge';
export const POLL_LOCK_MS = 300_000;
export const UNKNOWN_HOLD_MS = 600_000;
export const MAX_CANDIDATES_PER_POLL = 1;
export const MAX_SCAN_MS = 60_000;
