const ID = /^[a-f0-9]{24}$/;

// Only speech attributed to the owner within the ended call counts. Platform
// callbacks can be serialized as user messages after hang-up; exclude them.
function hasOwnerSpeech(transcript, startedAt, endedAt, nativeUtc) {
  return transcript.some((entry) => {
    if (entry?.role !== 'user' || typeof entry.text !== 'string' || !entry.text.trim()) return false;
    if (/^\s*\[(?:Outbound|Inbound) call\]/i.test(entry.text)) return false;
    const at = isoMs(entry.at, nativeUtc);
    return Number.isFinite(at) && at >= startedAt && at <= endedAt;
  });
}

function isoMs(value, nativeNaiveTimestampsAreUtc = false) {
  if (typeof value !== 'string') return NaN;
  if (/[zZ]|[+-]\d\d:\d\d$/.test(value)) return Date.parse(value);
  // Opt-in only: native responses have been observed without a zone; callers
  // must configure this after verifying the platform's timestamp policy.
  if (nativeNaiveTimestampsAreUtc && /^\d{4}-\d\d-\d\dT\d\d:\d\d:\d\d(?:\.\d+)?$/.test(value)) return Date.parse(`${value}Z`);
  return NaN;
}

function responseJson(value, status = 200) {
  return Response.json(value, { status, headers: { 'Cache-Control': 'no-store' } });
}

async function request(fetchImpl, url, token, init = {}, timeoutMs = 15_000) {
  return fetchImpl(url, {
    ...init,
    redirect: 'manual',
    signal: AbortSignal.timeout(timeoutMs),
    headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`, ...(init.body ? { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } : {}) },
  });
}

export async function findVisibleMessage(fetchImpl, token, conversationId, messageId) {
  if (!ID.test(conversationId) || !/^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{12}$/i.test(messageId)) return false;
  try {
    const response = await fetchImpl(`${BASE}/user-agent/conversations/${conversationId}/messages?limit=200`, {
      method: 'GET', redirect: 'manual', signal: AbortSignal.timeout(5_000), headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` },
    });
    if (!response.ok) return false;
    const data = await response.json();
    const messages = Array.isArray(data) ? data : data?.messages;
    return Array.isArray(messages) && messages.some((message) => message?.id === messageId && message?.role === 'user');
  } catch { return false; }
}

// A verified delivered message clears only the ambiguous dispatch state, not
// the cooldown. This never sends another message or places another call.
export async function reconcileCallDispatch(controls, fetchImpl, pat) {
  const rows = await controls.filter({ name: 'owner_native_call' }, '-created_date', 2);
  if (rows.length !== 1) return false;
  const row = rows[0];
  if (row.name !== 'owner_native_call' || !['unknown', 'submitting'].includes(row.dispatch_state)) return false;
  if (!await findVisibleMessage(fetchImpl, pat, row.conversation_id, row.message_id)) return false;
  const result = await controls.updateMany(
    { id: row.id, name: row.name, request_id: row.request_id, conversation_id: row.conversation_id, message_id: row.message_id, dispatch_state: row.dispatch_state },
    { $set: { dispatch_state: 'submitted' } },
  );
  return result?.success === true && result.updated === 1;
}

/** Confirm a slow native POST by observing the exact user message once it is visible.
 * No second POST is ever attempted. A visible exact UUID proves dispatch only, not execution. */
export async function postWithDeliveryConfirmation(fetchImpl, url, token, body, conversationId, messageId) {
  const monitorUrl = `${BASE}/user-agent/conversations/${conversationId}/messages?limit=20`;
  let stopped = false;
  let timer;
  const pause = (ms) => new Promise((resolve) => { timer = setTimeout(resolve, ms); });
  const postPromise = request(fetchImpl, url, token, { method: 'POST', body: JSON.stringify(body) }, 180_000);
  // Always observe rejection so a monitor win cannot create an unhandled rejection.
  postPromise.catch(() => {});
  const postOutcome = postPromise.then((response) => {
    if (response.ok) return { kind: 'accepted' };
    if (response.status >= 400 && response.status < 500 && response.status !== 408) return { kind: 'rejected', status: response.status };
    return { kind: 'unknown' };
  }, () => ({ kind: 'unknown' }));
  const monitor = (async () => {
    // Let an already-resolved POST win before starting any read monitor.
    await pause(1000);
    for (let attempt = 0; attempt < 3 && !stopped; attempt++) {
      if (attempt) await pause(1000);
      if (stopped) return { kind: 'cancelled' };
      try {
        const response = await fetchImpl(monitorUrl, {
          method: 'GET', redirect: 'manual', signal: AbortSignal.timeout(5_000),
          headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` },
        });
        if (!response.ok) {
          continue;
        }
        const data = await response.json();
        const messages = Array.isArray(data) ? data : data?.messages;
        if (Array.isArray(messages) && messages.some((message) => message?.id === messageId && message?.role === 'user')) return { kind: 'confirmed' };
      } catch { /* bounded read-only monitor; unresolved delivery remains ambiguous */ }
    }
    return { kind: 'unknown' };
  })();
  // A monitor timeout means only that the message is not visible yet. It must
  // not beat a still-running POST and manufacture an early `unknown` state.
  // Await the bounded POST outcome before declaring ambiguity; a confirmed
  // visible message remains an allowed early success path.
  const winner = await Promise.race([postOutcome, monitor]);
  if (winner.kind === 'unknown') {
    const outcome = await postOutcome;
    stopped = true;
    clearTimeout(timer);
    return outcome;
  }
  stopped = true;
  clearTimeout(timer);
  return winner;
}

/**
 * Run one read/dispatch pass. The caller supplies a Base44 service-role entity facade.
 * `pat` is the server-only JARVIS_NATIVE_TOKEN; it is never returned or logged.
 */
export async function runAfterCallPoll({ store, fetchImpl = fetch, pat, now = Date.now, enabled = true }) {
  if (!enabled || !pat) return { enabled: false, processed: 0, reason: 'disabled_or_missing_secret' };
  const controls = store.control;
  const receipts = store.receipt;
  const configured = await controls.filter({ name: CONTROL_NAME }, '-created_date', 2);
  if (configured.length !== 1 || configured[0].enabled !== true || !Number.isFinite(configured[0].enrollment_at_ms)) {
    return { enabled: false, processed: 0, reason: configured.length === 1 ? 'not_configured' : 'control_not_unique' };
  }
  const control = configured[0];
  const t = now();
  const scanDeadline = t + MAX_SCAN_MS;
  const requestId = crypto.randomUUID();
  const claim = await controls.updateMany(
    { id: control.id, name: CONTROL_NAME, poll_lock_until_ms: { $lte: t } },
    { $set: { poll_request_id: requestId, poll_lock_until_ms: t + POLL_LOCK_MS } },
  );
  if (!claim?.success || claim.updated !== 1) return { enabled: true, processed: 0, reason: 'poll_lock_busy' };

  try {
    await reconcileCallDispatch(controls, fetchImpl, pat);
    const auth = await request(fetchImpl, `${BASE}/auth/token`, pat);
    if (!auth.ok) return { enabled: true, processed: 0, reason: 'runtime_token_rejected' };
    const authData = await auth.json();
    const runtimeToken = typeof authData?.token === 'string' && authData.token ? authData.token : null;
    if (!runtimeToken) return { enabled: true, processed: 0, reason: 'runtime_token_missing' };
    const list = await request(fetchImpl, `${BASE}/phone/calls?limit=20&offset=0`, runtimeToken);
    if (!list.ok) return { enabled: true, processed: 0, reason: 'call_list_failed' };
    const calls = (await list.json())?.calls;
    if (!Array.isArray(calls)) return { enabled: true, processed: 0, reason: 'call_list_invalid' };
    let processed = 0;
    let candidates = 0;
    let reconciliations = 0;
    for (const call of calls) {
      if (now() >= scanDeadline) return { enabled: true, processed, reason: 'scan_deadline' };
      if (call?.is_owner !== true || !['completed', 'failed'].includes(call.status) || typeof call.id !== 'string' || !ID.test(call.id)) continue;
      const startedAt = isoMs(call.started_at, control.native_naive_timestamps_utc === true);
      const completedAt = isoMs(call.ended_at || call.completed_at, control.native_naive_timestamps_utc === true);
      if (!Number.isFinite(startedAt) || !Number.isFinite(completedAt) || startedAt < control.enrollment_at_ms || completedAt < startedAt || completedAt > now()) continue;
      if (candidates >= MAX_CANDIDATES_PER_POLL) break;
      const existing = await receipts.filter({ call_id: call.id }, '-created_date', 1);
      if (existing.length) {
        const prior = existing[0];
        if (reconciliations < 1 && ['dispatch_submitting', 'dispatch_unknown'].includes(prior.status)) {
          reconciliations++;
          if (await findVisibleMessage(fetchImpl, pat, prior.conversation_id, prior.message_id)) {
            await receipts.update(prior.id, { status: 'dispatch_accepted' });
          }
        }
        continue;
      }
      const detailResponse = await request(fetchImpl, `${BASE}/phone/calls/${call.id}`, runtimeToken);
      if (!detailResponse.ok) continue;
      const detail = await detailResponse.json();
      const transcript = detail?.transcript;
      if (detail?.call?.id !== call.id || detail.call.is_owner !== true || !['completed', 'failed'].includes(detail.call.status)) continue;
      if (!Array.isArray(transcript) || !hasOwnerSpeech(transcript, startedAt, completedAt, control.native_naive_timestamps_utc === true)) continue;
      candidates++;

      // Renew and fence before creating a durable dispatch receipt.
      const renewed = await controls.updateMany(
        { id: control.id, poll_request_id: requestId, poll_lock_until_ms: { $gt: now() } },
        { $set: { poll_lock_until_ms: now() + POLL_LOCK_MS } },
      );
      if (now() >= scanDeadline) return { enabled: true, processed, reason: 'scan_deadline' };
      if (!renewed?.success || renewed.updated !== 1) return { enabled: true, processed, reason: 'poll_lease_lost' };
      // Receipt first: a crash after this point must not turn into an automatic replay.
      const receipt = await receipts.create({ call_id: call.id, status: 'dispatch_pending', call_completed_at_ms: completedAt, created_at_ms: t });
      if (!receipt?.id) continue;
      let conversationId;
      try {
        const beforeDispatch = await controls.updateMany(
          { id: control.id, poll_request_id: requestId, poll_lock_until_ms: { $gt: now() } },
          { $set: { poll_lock_until_ms: now() + POLL_LOCK_MS } },
        );
        if (!beforeDispatch?.success || beforeDispatch.updated !== 1) {
          await receipts.update(receipt.id, { status: 'dispatch_deferred', error: 'poll_lease_lost' });
          return { enabled: true, processed, reason: 'poll_lease_lost' };
        }
        // Native owner messaging is authenticated with the server-only scoped PAT, as in callMe.
        // The short-lived runtime token is intentionally limited to phone read endpoints.
        const conversation = await request(fetchImpl, `${BASE}/user-agent/conversations/v2`, pat, { method: 'POST', body: JSON.stringify({ metadata: { source: 'after_call_bridge', call_id: call.id } }) });
        if (!conversation.ok) {
          await receipts.update(receipt.id, { status: 'dispatch_failed', error: 'conversation_create_failed' });
          continue;
        }
        const conversationData = await conversation.json();
        conversationId = conversationData?.id;
        if (typeof conversationId !== 'string' || !ID.test(conversationId)) throw new Error('invalid_conversation_id');
        const messageId = crypto.randomUUID();
        await receipts.update(receipt.id, { status: 'dispatch_submitting', conversation_id: conversationId, message_id: messageId });
        const delivery = await postWithDeliveryConfirmation(fetchImpl, `${BASE}/user-agent/conversations/${conversationId}/messages`, pat, {
          id: messageId, role: 'user', hidden: false, content: TRANSCRIPT_PROMPT.replace('{{CALL_ID}}', call.id).replace('{{CALL_STATUS}}', detail.call.status),
        }, conversationId, messageId);
        if (delivery.kind === 'unknown') {
          await receipts.update(receipt.id, { status: 'dispatch_unknown', lock_until_ms: now() + UNKNOWN_HOLD_MS });
          continue;
        }
        if (delivery.kind === 'rejected') {
          await receipts.update(receipt.id, { status: 'dispatch_failed', error: 'message_rejected' });
          continue;
        }
        await receipts.update(receipt.id, { status: 'dispatch_accepted' });
        processed++;
      } catch {
        // Never retry an ambiguous message submission automatically.
        await receipts.update(receipt.id, { status: 'dispatch_unknown', lock_until_ms: now() + UNKNOWN_HOLD_MS });
      }
    }
    return { enabled: true, processed, reason: 'ok' };
  } finally {
    await controls.updateMany({ id: control.id, poll_request_id: requestId }, { $set: { poll_lock_until_ms: 0 } });
  }
}

/** Admin-session-only until Base44 automation caller auth is proven. Never trusts a header. */
export async function handleAfterCallPoll(req, { createClientFromRequest, secrets, fetchImpl = fetch }) {
  if (req.method !== 'POST') return responseJson({ ok: false, error: 'method_not_allowed' }, 405);
  let base44, user;
  try { base44 = createClientFromRequest(req); user = await base44.auth.me(); } catch { return responseJson({ ok: false, error: 'unauthorized' }, 401); }
  if (!user) return responseJson({ ok: false, error: 'unauthorized' }, 401);
  if (user.role !== 'admin') return responseJson({ ok: false, error: 'forbidden' }, 403);
  const pat = secrets.get('JARVIS_NATIVE_TOKEN');
  const result = await runAfterCallPoll({ store: { control: base44.asServiceRole.entities.CallControl, receipt: base44.asServiceRole.entities.CallReceipt }, fetchImpl, pat });
  return responseJson(result);
}



===== FILE: source/base44/shared/hud.ts =====
// Shared helpers for Jarvis HUD backend functions.

import { createClientFromRequest } from 'npm:@base44/sdk@0.8.44';
import { secrets } from 'base44:runtime';

export const MAX_BODY_BYTES = 100 * 1024;

export function method405(req) {
  if (req.method !== 'POST') {
    return new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: 'method not allowed, use POST' }), {
      status: 405,
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }
    });
  }
  return null;
}

export async function readBody(req) {
  let raw;
  try {
    raw = await req.text();
  } catch (e) {
    return { ok: false, status: 400, error: 'invalid request body' };
  }
  if (raw.length > MAX_BODY_BYTES) {
    return { ok: false, status: 413, error: 'request body too large' };
  }
  if (!raw) return { ok: true, body: {} };
  try {
    return { ok: true, body: JSON.parse(raw) };
  } catch (e) {
    return { ok: false, status: 400, error: 'invalid JSON' };
  }
}

export function normalizeKey(s) {
  return String(s == null ? '' : s).trim().toLowerCase().replace(/\s+/g, ' ');
}

export function taskKey(title) {
  return normalizeKey(title);
}

export function leadKey(businessName, suburb) {
  return normalizeKey(businessName) + '|' + normalizeKey(suburb);
}

export function todayISO() {
  const d = new Date();
  const y = d.getFullYear();
  const m = String(d.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0');
  const day = String(d.getDate()).padStart(2, '0');
  return `${y}-${m}-${day}`;
}

const WEEKDAYS = ['sunday', 'monday', 'tuesday', 'wednesday', 'thursday', 'friday', 'saturday'];

// Current calendar date in Australia/Brisbane (UTC+10, no DST) as a UTC-midnight Date.
function brisbaneBase() {
  const now = new Date();
  const t = new Date(now.getTime() + 10 * 3600 * 1000);
  return new Date(Date.UTC(t.getUTCFullYear(), t.getUTCMonth(), t.getUTCDate()));
}

function isoFromUTC(base) {
  const y = base.getUTCFullYear();
  const m = String(base.getUTCMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0');
  const d = String(base.getUTCDate()).padStart(2, '0');
  return `${y}-${m}-${d}`;
}

function validDate(y, mo, d) {
  const dt = new Date(Date.UTC(y, mo - 1, d));
  return dt.getUTCFullYear() === y && dt.getUTCMonth() === mo - 1 && dt.getUTCDate() === d;
}

export function normalizeDate(input) {
  if (typeof input !== 'string') return { ok: false, error: 'due_date must be a string' };
  const s = input.trim().toLowerCase();
  if (!s) return { ok: false, error: 'due_date is empty' };

  let m = s.match(/^(\d{4})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})$/);
  if (m) {
    const y = +m[1], mo = +m[2], d = +m[3];
    if (!validDate(y, mo, d)) return { ok: false, error: 'invalid date' };
    return { ok: true, iso: `${m[1]}-${m[2]}-${m[3]}` };
  }

  m = s.match(/^(\d{1,2})\/(\d{1,2})\/(\d{4})$/);
  if (m) {
    const d = +m[1], mo = +m[2], y = +m[3];
    if (!validDate(y, mo, d)) return { ok: false, error: 'invalid date' };
    return { ok: true, iso: `${y}-${String(mo).padStart(2, '0')}-${String(d).padStart(2, '0')}` };
  }

  const base = brisbaneBase();
  if (s === 'today') return { ok: true, iso: isoFromUTC(base) };
  if (s === 'tomorrow') return { ok: true, iso: isoFromUTC(new Date(base.getTime() + 86400000)) };
  if (s === 'next week') return { ok: true, iso: isoFromUTC(new Date(base.getTime() + 7 * 86400000)) };

  m = s.match(/^next\s+(sunday|monday|tuesday|wednesday|thursday|friday|saturday)$/);
  if (m) {
    const target = WEEKDAYS.indexOf(m[1]);
    const today = base.getUTCDay();
    let diff = (target - today + 7) % 7;
    if (diff === 0) diff = 7;
    return { ok: true, iso: isoFromUTC(new Date(base.getTime() + diff * 86400000)) };
  }

  return { ok: false, error: 'due_date must be YYYY-MM-DD, DD/MM/YYYY, today, tomorrow, next <weekday> or next week' };
}

export function validateEnum(value, allowed, label) {
  if (!allowed.includes(value)) {
    return { ok: false, error: `${label} must be one of: ${allowed.join('|')}` };
  }
  return { ok: true };
}

// Find all records whose normalized key matches, oldest first.
export async function findMatches(entityColl, keyFn, key) {
  const all = await entityColl.list('-created_date', 500);
  return all
    .filter((r) => keyFn(r) === key)
    .sort((a, b) => new Date(a.created_date) - new Date(b.created_date));
}

// Keep the oldest match, delete the rest. Swallows delete errors.
export async function dedupeMatches(entityColl, matches) {
  for (const r of matches.slice(1)) {
    try { await entityColl.delete(r.id); } catch (e) {}
  }
}

// Brisbane today as YYYY-MM-DD (UTC+10, no DST).
export function brisbaneTodayISO() {
  const now = new Date();
  const t = new Date(now.getTime() + 10 * 3600 * 1000);
  const y = t.getUTCFullYear();
  const m = String(t.getUTCMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0');
  const d = String(t.getUTCDate()).padStart(2, '0');
  return `${y}-${m}-${d}`;
}

// Authorize a write: either a valid x-hud-key (external caller) OR a
// server-verified Base44 admin/owner session. Never trusts browser-supplied
// identity. Returns { ok, base44, user } on success, { ok:false, status } otherwise.
export async function authorizeWrite(req) {
  const expected = secrets.get("HUD_KEY");
  const provided = req.headers.get("x-hud-key");
  const hudAuthed = !!(expected && provided && provided === expected);

  const base44 = createClientFromRequest(req);
  if (hudAuthed) return { ok: true, base44, user: null };

  try {
    const me = await base44.auth.me();
    if (me) {
      if (me.role === 'admin') return { ok: true, base44, user: me };
      const ownerEmail = secrets.get("OWNER_EMAIL");
      if (ownerEmail && me.email && String(me.email).toLowerCase() === String(ownerEmail).toLowerCase()) {
        return { ok: true, base44, user: me };
      }
    }
  } catch (e) { /* not authenticated */ }
  return { ok: false, status: 401 };
}

===== FILE: source/src/App.jsx =====
import { Toaster } from "@/components/ui/toaster"
import { QueryClientProvider } from '@tanstack/react-query'
import { queryClientInstance } from '@/lib/query-client'
import { BrowserRouter as Router, Route, Routes, Navigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import PageNotFound from './lib/PageNotFound';
import { AuthProvider, useAuth } from '@/lib/AuthContext';
import UserNotRegisteredError from '@/components/UserNotRegisteredError';
import ScrollToTop from './components/ScrollToTop';
import ProtectedRoute from '@/components/ProtectedRoute';
import Login from '@/pages/Login';
import Register from '@/pages/Register';
import ForgotPassword from '@/pages/ForgotPassword';
import ResetPassword from '@/pages/ResetPassword';
// Add page imports here
import Today from '@/pages/Today';

const AuthenticatedApp = () => {
  const { isLoadingAuth, isLoadingPublicSettings, authError, navigateToLogin } = useAuth();

  // Show loading spinner while checking app public settings or auth
  if (isLoadingPublicSettings || isLoadingAuth) {
    return (
      <div className="fixed inset-0 flex items-center justify-center">
        <div className="w-8 h-8 border-4 border-slate-200 border-t-slate-800 rounded-full animate-spin"></div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  // Handle authentication errors
  if (authError) {
    if (authError.type === 'user_not_registered') {
      return <UserNotRegisteredError />;
    } else if (authError.type === 'auth_required') {
      // Redirect to login automatically
      navigateToLogin();
      return null;
    }
  }

  // Render the main app
  return (
    <Routes>
      <Route path="/login" element={<Login />} />
      <Route path="/register" element={<Register />} />
      <Route path="/forgot-password" element={<ForgotPassword />} />
      <Route path="/reset-password" element={<ResetPassword />} />
      <Route element={<ProtectedRoute unauthenticatedElement={<Navigate to="/login" replace />} />}>
        <Route path="/" element={<Today />} />
      </Route>
      <Route path="*" element={<PageNotFound />} />
    </Routes>
  );
};


function App() {

  return (
    <AuthProvider>
      <QueryClientProvider client={queryClientInstance}>
        <Router>
          <ScrollToTop />
          <AuthenticatedApp />
        </Router>
        <Toaster />
      </QueryClientProvider>
    </AuthProvider>
  )
}

export default App

===== FILE: source/src/components/ProtectedRoute.jsx =====
import { useEffect } from 'react';
import { Outlet } from 'react-router-dom';
import { useAuth } from '@/lib/AuthContext';
import UserNotRegisteredError from '@/components/UserNotRegisteredError';

const DefaultFallback = () => (
  <div className="fixed inset-0 flex items-center justify-center">
    <div className="w-8 h-8 border-4 border-slate-200 border-t-slate-800 rounded-full animate-spin"></div>
  </div>
);

export default function ProtectedRoute({ fallback = <DefaultFallback />, unauthenticatedElement }) {
  const { isAuthenticated, isLoadingAuth, authChecked, authError, checkUserAuth } = useAuth();

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!authChecked && !isLoadingAuth) {
      checkUserAuth();
    }
  }, [authChecked, isLoadingAuth, checkUserAuth]);

  if (isLoadingAuth || !authChecked) {
    return fallback;
  }

  if (authError) {
    if (authError.type === 'user_not_registered') {
      return <UserNotRegisteredError />;
    }
    return unauthenticatedElement;
  }

  if (!isAuthenticated) {
    return unauthenticatedElement;
  }

  return <Outlet />;
}


===== FILE: source/src/components/ScrollToTop.jsx =====
import { useEffect } from "react";
import { useLocation, useNavigationType } from "react-router-dom";

const getHashId = (hash) => {
  const rawId = hash.slice(1);

  try {
    return decodeURIComponent(rawId);
  } catch {
    return rawId;
  }
};

export default function ScrollToTop() {
  const { pathname, hash } = useLocation();
  const navigationType = useNavigationType();

  useEffect(() => {
    if (navigationType === "POP") return;

    if (hash) {
      const id = getHashId(hash);
      const timer = window.setTimeout(() => {
        document.getElementById(id)?.scrollIntoView({ behavior: "smooth" });
      }, 50);
      return () => window.clearTimeout(timer);
    }

    window.scrollTo({ top: 0, left: 0, behavior: "instant" });
  }, [pathname, hash, navigationType]);

  return null;
}

===== FILE: source/src/components/jarvis/ActivityCharts.jsx =====
import React, { useMemo } from 'react';
import { HUD, lastBrisbaneDays, brisbaneDayKey, parseBase44Date } from '@/lib/jarvis';

export default function ActivityCharts({ logs, tasks }) {
  // Sparkline: ActivityLog record counts per hour over the past 24 hours.
  const hourly = useMemo(() => {
    const now = Date.now();
    const buckets = new Array(24).fill(0);
    for (const l of logs || []) {
      const parsed = parseBase44Date(l.created_date);
      const t = parsed?.kind === 'instant' ? parsed.epochMs : NaN;
      const diff = now - t;
      if (diff >= 0 && diff < 24 * 3600 * 1000) {
        const idx = 23 - Math.floor(diff / 3600000);
        if (idx >= 0 && idx < 24) buckets[idx]++;
      }
    }
    return buckets;
  }, [logs]);

  const maxH = Math.max(1, ...hourly);
  const sparkPath = useMemo(() => {
    const w = 200, h = 40;
    return hourly.map((c, i) => {
      const x = (i / 23) * w;
      const y = h - (c / maxH) * h;
      return `${i === 0 ? 'M' : 'L'} ${x.toFixed(1)} ${y.toFixed(1)}`;
    }).join(' ');
  }, [hourly, maxH]);

  // Bar chart: tasks done per day for the last 14 Brisbane days (using done_at).
  const days = useMemo(() => lastBrisbaneDays(14), []);
  const counts = useMemo(() => {
    const map = Object.fromEntries(days.map((d) => [d, 0]));
    for (const t of tasks || []) {
      if (t.status === 'done' && t.done_at) {
        const k = brisbaneDayKey(t.done_at);
        if (map[k] !== undefined) map[k]++;
      }
    }
    return days.map((d) => map[d]);
  }, [days, tasks]);
  const maxC = Math.max(1, ...counts);

  return (
    <div>
      <div className="font-mono text-[13px] uppercase tracking-[0.15em] text-hud-text2 pb-2">
        ACTIVITY · LAST 24H
      </div>
      <div className="h-px bg-hud-border mb-3" />
      <svg viewBox="0 0 200 40" className="w-full" style={{ height: 40 }} preserveAspectRatio="none">
        <path d={sparkPath} fill="none" stroke={HUD.accent} strokeWidth="1" opacity="0.7" />
      </svg>

      <div className="mt-4">
        <div className="font-mono text-[10px] uppercase tracking-[0.14em] text-hud-faint mb-2">
          TASKS DONE · 14D
        </div>
        <div className="flex items-end gap-1" style={{ height: 48 }}>
          {counts.map((c, i) => (
            <div key={i} className="flex-1 flex items-end" style={{ height: 48 }}>
              <div
                style={{
                  width: '100%',
                  height: `${(c / maxC) * 100}%`,
                  minHeight: c > 0 ? 2 : 1,
                  background: c >= 3 ? HUD.amber : HUD.accent,
                  opacity: c > 0 ? 0.9 : 0.25
                }}
              />
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

import React, { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { HUD, relTime, formatActivityLog } from '@/lib/jarvis';

const TYPE_LABEL = {
  task_done: 'TASK DONE',
  task_started: 'TASK STARTED',
  note: 'NOTE',
  update: 'UPDATE'
};

export default function CommsChannel({ logs }) {
  const [, setClock] = useState(() => Date.now());
  useEffect(() => { const timer = setInterval(() => setClock(Date.now()), 30_000); return () => clearInterval(timer); }, []);
  const now = Date.now();
  const rows = (logs || []).map((log) => ({ log, formatted: formatActivityLog(log) })).filter(({ formatted }) => !formatted.hidden).slice(0, 6);
  return (
    <div>
      <div className="font-mono text-[13px] uppercase tracking-[0.15em] text-hud-text2 pb-2">
        LIVE CHANNEL · COMMS
      </div>
      <div className="h-px bg-hud-border mb-3" />
      <div className="flex flex-col gap-3">
        {rows.map(({ log, formatted }) => (
          <div key={log.id} className="flex gap-3 min-w-0">
            <span className="w-[3px] self-stretch shrink-0" style={{ background: HUD.amber }} />
            <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
              <div className="font-mono text-[10px] uppercase tracking-[0.14em] text-hud-faint">
                {TYPE_LABEL[log.type] || 'ACTIVITY'} · {relTime(log.created_date, now)}
              </div>
              <div
                className="font-body text-[15px] text-hud-text2 leading-snug"
                style={{
                  display: '-webkit-box',
                  WebkitLineClamp: 2,
                  WebkitBoxOrient: 'vertical',
                  overflow: 'hidden'
                }}
              >
                {formatted.message}
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        ))}
        {rows.length === 0 && (
          <div className="font-mono text-[12px] text-hud-faint">no comms yet</div>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

import React from 'react';

// Fixed full-screen film grain generated inline with an SVG feTurbulence
// filter. No image file, no external asset. Sits above the page background
// and below all content; pointer-events disabled; opacity drifts faintly.
export default function GrainOverlay() {
  return (
    <svg
      className="fixed inset-0 w-full h-full pointer-events-none"
      style={{ zIndex: 0 }}
      aria-hidden="true"
    >
      <filter id="hud-grain-filter">
        <feTurbulence
          type="fractalNoise"
          baseFrequency="0.9"
          numOctaves="2"
          stitchTiles="stitch"
        />
      </filter>
      <rect
        className="hud-grain"
        width="100%"
        height="100%"
        filter="url(#hud-grain-filter)"
        opacity="0.02"
      />
    </svg>
  );
}

===== FILE: source/src/components/jarvis/HeaderActions.jsx =====
import React, { useState, useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
import { useAuth } from '@/lib/AuthContext';
import { base44 } from '@/api/base44Client';

const TG_URL = '__STUDENT_TELEGRAM_URL__';
const TG_READY = /^https:\/\/t\.me\/[A-Za-z0-9_]+$/.test(TG_URL);
const TOAST_MS = 5000;
const POLL_MS = 3000;
const MAX_POLL_MS = 180000;
const COOLDOWN_MS = 60000;
const btnBase = 'font-mono text-[10px] tracking-[0.18em] uppercase px-2.5 py-1.5 border border-hud-border bg-transparent text-hud-text2 hover:bg-hud-panel hover:text-hud-text transition-colors duration-200 focus:outline-none focus-visible:border-hud-accent inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 disabled:opacity-60';

function prefersReducedMotion() { return typeof window !== 'undefined' && window.matchMedia && window.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches; }
export function cooldownSeconds(deadlineMs, nowMs = Date.now()) {
  if (!Number.isFinite(deadlineMs) || deadlineMs <= nowMs) return 0;
  return Math.max(0, Math.ceil((deadlineMs - nowMs) / 1000));
}

export default function HeaderActions() {
  const { user } = useAuth();
  const [state, setState] = useState('idle');
  const [toast, setToast] = useState(false);
  const [toastMsg, setToastMsg] = useState('');
  const [toastRed, setToastRed] = useState(false);
  const pendingRef = useRef(false);
  const timer = useRef(null);
  const pollTimer = useRef(null);
  const mounted = useRef(true);
  const [cooldownUntil, setCooldownUntil] = useState(0);
  const [, refreshClock] = useState(() => Date.now());
  const cooldown = cooldownSeconds(cooldownUntil);
  const reduced = prefersReducedMotion();
  useEffect(() => { mounted.current = true; return () => { mounted.current = false; if (timer.current) clearTimeout(timer.current); if (pollTimer.current) clearTimeout(pollTimer.current); }; }, []);
  useEffect(() => {
    const refresh = () => refreshClock(Date.now());
    const id = setInterval(refresh, 1000);
    document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', refresh);
    window.addEventListener('focus', refresh);
    return () => { clearInterval(id); document.removeEventListener('visibilitychange', refresh); window.removeEventListener('focus', refresh); };
  }, []);
  const showToast = (msg, red = false) => { if (!mounted.current) return; setToastMsg(msg); setToastRed(red); setToast(true); if (timer.current) clearTimeout(timer.current); timer.current = setTimeout(() => setToast(false), TOAST_MS); };
  const showUnknown = () => { if (!mounted.current) return; setState('unknown'); setCooldownUntil(Date.now() + 600_000); showToast('Request status unknown. Check Jarvis before retrying.', true); };
  const poll = async (requestId) => {
    const started = Date.now();
    while (Date.now() - started < MAX_POLL_MS) {
      await new Promise((resolve) => { pollTimer.current = setTimeout(resolve, POLL_MS); });
      if (!mounted.current) return;
      let response, data;
      try { response = await base44.functions.invoke('callStatus', { request_id: requestId }); data = response?.data; } catch (e) { response = e?.response; data = response?.data; }
      if (!mounted.current) return;
      if (data?.state === 'submitted') { setState('submitted'); setCooldownUntil(Date.now() + COOLDOWN_MS); showToast('Call request sent to Jarvis.'); return; }
      if (data?.state === 'failed') { setState('failed'); showToast('Call request was not accepted. Check the call service.', true); return; }
      if (data?.state === 'unknown' || response?.status === 409) { showUnknown(); return; }
      if (response?.status === 401) { setState('failed'); showToast('Sign in to check call status.', true); return; }
      if (response?.status === 403) { setState('failed'); showToast('Admin only.', true); return; }
      if (!response || response.status >= 400) { showUnknown(); return; }
      setState('preparing');
    }
    showUnknown();
  };
  const handleCallMe = async () => {
    if (pendingRef.current || cooldown > 0 || !mounted.current) return;
    pendingRef.current = true; setState('queued'); showToast('Requesting a call…');
    try {
      const response = await base44.functions.invoke('callMe', {});
      if (!mounted.current) return;
      const data = response?.data;
      if (response?.status !== 202 || !data?.request_id) { showUnknown(); return; }
      showToast('Call request queued. Jarvis is preparing your call.');
      await poll(data.request_id);
    } catch (e) {
      if (!mounted.current) return;
      const status = e?.response?.status;
      const known = ['native_token_not_configured', 'control_not_initialized', 'control_not_unique', 'call_control_unavailable'].includes(e?.response?.data?.error);
      if (status === 401 || status === 403 || status === 429 || known) {
        setState('failed');
        if (status === 429) setCooldownUntil(Date.now() + 60_000);
        showToast(status === 401 ? 'Sign in to call.' : status === 403 ? 'Admin only.' : status === 429 ? 'Cooldown active — wait a moment.' : 'Call service is unavailable. Check Jarvis settings.', true);
      } else showUnknown();
    }
    finally { pendingRef.current = false; }
  };
  const ownerLabel = user?.full_name ? user.full_name.trim().split(/\s+/)[0].toUpperCase() : 'OWNER';
  return <div className="relative ml-auto flex items-center gap-2">
    <span className="font-mono text-[10px] tracking-[0.18em] uppercase text-hud-faint px-1">{ownerLabel}</span>
    <button type="button" onClick={handleCallMe} disabled={pendingRef.current || state === 'queued' || state === 'preparing' || cooldown > 0} className={btnBase}>{state === 'queued' ? 'Requesting…' : state === 'preparing' ? 'Preparing…' : cooldown > 0 ? `Cooldown ${cooldown}s` : 'Call Me'}</button>
    <button type="button" disabled={!TG_READY} onClick={() => TG_READY && window.open(TG_URL, '_blank', 'noopener,noreferrer')} className={btnBase}>Text Jarvis</button>
    <div aria-live="polite" className="absolute right-0 top-full mt-2 z-50 pointer-events-none" style={{ opacity: toast ? 1 : 0, transform: toast ? 'translateY(0)' : 'translateY(-4px)', transition: reduced ? 'none' : 'opacity 200ms ease-out, transform 200ms ease-out' }}>
      {toast && <div className="max-w-[320px] font-mono text-[10px] tracking-[0.12em] bg-hud-panel border px-3 py-1.5 text-right" style={{ color: toastRed ? '#E5533D' : '#EAF4FF', borderColor: toastRed ? 'rgba(229,83,61,0.5)' : '#14356B' }}>{toastMsg}</div>}
    </div>
  </div>;
}

    const t = setInterval(() => setNow(new Date()), 1000);
    return () => clearInterval(t);
  }, []);

  const objective = goal && String(goal).trim() ? String(goal).trim() : 'set your 90-day goal';

  return (
    <div className="h-16 flex items-center gap-6 border-b border-hud-border">
      <span className="hud-pulse w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-hud-accent" />

      <span className="w-px h-4 bg-hud-border" />

        style={{ border: '1px solid rgba(232,180,90,0.5)' }}
      >
        OBJECTIVE · {objective}
      </span>
      <HeaderActions />

===== FILE: source/src/components/jarvis/HudRing.jsx =====
import React, { useState, useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
import { HUD } from '@/lib/jarvis';
import { useCountUp } from '@/hooks/useCountUp';

function polar(r, angleDeg) {
  const a = ((angleDeg - 90) * Math.PI) / 180;
  return { x: 160 + r * Math.cos(a), y: 160 + r * Math.sin(a) };
}

function arcPath(r, centerDeg, spanDeg) {
  const a1 = centerDeg - spanDeg / 2;
  const a2 = centerDeg + spanDeg / 2;
  const p1 = polar(r, a1);
  const p2 = polar(r, a2);
  return `M ${p1.x} ${p1.y} A ${r} ${r} 0 0 1 ${p2.x} ${p2.y}`;
}

const ARCS = [45, 135, 225, 315];
const RING_C = [45, 135, 225, 315];

export default function HudRing({ doneToday, touchedToday, pulseKey }) {
  const displayCount = useCountUp(doneToday);
  const ticks = Array.from({ length: 72 }, (_, i) => i);
  const litCount = touchedToday > 0 ? Math.round((doneToday / touchedToday) * 72) : 0;

  const ringA = Array.from({ length: 36 }, (_, i) => i);
  const ringD = Array.from({ length: 120 }, (_, i) => i);

  const radarStart = polar(118, 0);
  const radarEnd = polar(118, 50);
  const radarPath = `M ${radarStart.x} ${radarStart.y} A 118 118 0 0 1 ${radarEnd.x} ${radarEnd.y}`;

  const [ripples, setRipples] = useState([]);
  const [glowKey, setGlowKey] = useState(0);
  const rid = useRef(0);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (pulseKey === 0) return;
    const id = ++rid.current;
    setRipples((rs) => [...rs, id]);
    setGlowKey((k) => k + 1);
    const t = setTimeout(() => {
      setRipples((rs) => rs.filter((x) => x !== id));
    }, 1400);
    return () => clearTimeout(t);
  }, [pulseKey]);

  return (
    <div className="relative w-[320px] h-[320px] max-[600px]:w-[220px] max-[600px]:h-[220px]">
      <div
        className="hud-breathe-glow absolute pointer-events-none"
        style={{
          width: 180,
          height: 180,
          left: '50%',
          top: '50%',
          marginLeft: -90,
          marginTop: -90,
          background:
            'radial-gradient(circle, rgba(82,181,255,0.22) 0%, transparent 70%)'
        }}
      />
      <svg viewBox="0 0 320 320" className="absolute inset-0 w-full h-full">
        <defs>
          <linearGradient
            id="hud-radar-grad"
            gradientUnits="userSpaceOnUse"
            x1={radarStart.x}
            y1={radarStart.y}
            x2={radarEnd.x}
            y2={radarEnd.y}
          >
            <stop offset="0%" stopColor={HUD.accent} stopOpacity="0" />
            <stop offset="100%" stopColor={HUD.accent} stopOpacity="0.5" />
          </linearGradient>
        </defs>

        <g stroke={HUD.accent} fill="none">
          <circle cx="160" cy="160" r="150" strokeWidth="1" opacity="0.35" />
          <circle cx="160" cy="160" r="144" strokeWidth="1" opacity="0.14" strokeDasharray="1 14" className="hud-spin" />
          <circle cx="160" cy="160" r="100" strokeWidth="1" opacity="0.4" className="hud-breathe-circle" />
        </g>

        <g stroke={HUD.accent} fill="none" className="hud-ring-a" opacity="0.18">
          {ringA.map((i) => {
            const p1 = polar(121, i * 10);
            const p2 = polar(127, i * 10);
            return <line key={i} x1={p1.x} y1={p1.y} x2={p2.x} y2={p2.y} strokeWidth="1" />;
          })}
        </g>

        <circle cx="160" cy="160" r="112" stroke={HUD.accent} strokeWidth="1" fill="none" strokeDasharray="2 10" opacity="0.14" className="hud-ring-b" />

        <g stroke={HUD.accent} fill="none" className="hud-ring-c" opacity="0.22">
          {RING_C.map((deg) => (
            <path key={deg} d={arcPath(140, deg, 40)} strokeWidth="1" />
          ))}
        </g>

        <g fill={HUD.accent} className="hud-ring-d" opacity="0.12">
          {ringD.map((i) => {
            const p = polar(156, i * 3);
            return <circle key={i} cx={p.x} cy={p.y} r="0.7" />;
          })}
        </g>

        <g stroke={HUD.accent} fill="none">
          {ticks.map((i) => {
            const isMajor = i % 6 === 0;
            const r1 = isMajor ? 118 : 124;
            const p1 = polar(r1, i * 5);
            const p2 = polar(134, i * 5);
            const lit = i < litCount;
            const opacity = lit ? 0.9 : isMajor ? 0.3 : 0.13;
            const width = isMajor ? 1.2 : 1;
            return (
              <line key={i} x1={p1.x} y1={p1.y} x2={p2.x} y2={p2.y} strokeWidth={width} opacity={opacity} />
            );
          })}
        </g>

        <g stroke={HUD.accent} fill="none">
          {ARCS.map((deg) => {
            const r = 152;
            const a1 = ((deg - 14 - 90) * Math.PI) / 180;
            const a2 = ((deg + 14 - 90) * Math.PI) / 180;
            const x1 = 160 + r * Math.cos(a1);
            const y1 = 160 + r * Math.sin(a1);
            const x2 = 160 + r * Math.cos(a2);
            const y2 = 160 + r * Math.sin(a2);
            const d = `M ${x1} ${y1} A ${r} ${r} 0 0 1 ${x2} ${y2}`;
            const e1 = polar(r, deg - 14);
            const e2 = polar(r, deg + 14);
            return (
              <g key={deg}>
                <path d={d} strokeWidth="1.2" opacity="0.34" strokeLinecap="round" />
                <circle cx={e1.x} cy={e1.y} r="2" fill={HUD.accent} stroke="none" opacity="0.6" />
                <circle cx={e2.x} cy={e2.y} r="2" fill={HUD.accent} stroke="none" opacity="0.6" />
              </g>
            );
          })}
        </g>

        <g className="hud-radar">
          <path d={radarPath} stroke="url(#hud-radar-grad)" strokeWidth="1.5" fill="none" />
        </g>

        {ripples.map((id) => (
          <circle key={id} className="hud-ripple" cx="160" cy="160" r="100" fill="none" stroke={HUD.accent} strokeWidth="1.5" />
        ))}

        <text
          key={glowKey}
          x="160"
          y="158"
          textAnchor="middle"
          fontFamily="'Geist Mono', 'JetBrains Mono', monospace"
          fontSize="64"
          fill={HUD.text}
          className={glowKey > 0 ? 'hud-num-glow' : undefined}
        >
          {displayCount}
        </text>
        <text
          x="160"
          y="190"
          textAnchor="middle"
          fontFamily="'Geist Mono', 'JetBrains Mono', monospace"
          fontSize="13"
          letterSpacing="0.15em"
          fill={HUD.text2}
        >
          DONE TODAY
        </text>
      </svg>
    </div>
  );
}

import React, { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { HUD, fmtClock } from '@/lib/jarvis';

const kindColor = {
  fact: HUD.faint,
  decision: HUD.amber,
  outcome: HUD.green,
  question: HUD.accent,
  event: HUD.faint
};

export default function MemoryView({ memories }) {
  const [, setClock] = useState(() => Date.now());
  useEffect(() => { const timer = setInterval(() => setClock(Date.now()), 30_000); return () => clearInterval(timer); }, []);
  return (
    <div>
      <div className="font-mono text-[13px] uppercase tracking-[0.15em] text-hud-text2 pb-2">
        MEMORY
      </div>
      <div className="h-px bg-hud-border mb-3" />
      <div className="h-[320px] overflow-y-auto flex flex-col gap-[14px]">
        {(memories || []).map((m) => (
          <div key={m.id} className="flex items-center gap-3 min-w-0">
            <span className="font-mono text-[15px] text-hud-faint tabular-nums shrink-0">
              {fmtClock(m.created_date)}

              className="font-mono text-[11px] uppercase tracking-[0.1em] shrink-0"
              style={{ color: kindColor[m.kind] || HUD.faint }}

              {m.kind}

              {typeof m.text === 'string' && m.text.trim() ? m.text : 'unknown'}

      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

===== FILE: source/src/components/jarvis/Reactor.jsx =====
import React, { useId, useState } from 'react';
import './Reactor.css';

const CYAN = '#79edff';
const polar = (r, deg) => {
  const a = (deg - 90) * Math.PI / 180;
  return [320 + r * Math.cos(a), 320 + r * Math.sin(a)];
};
const arc = (r, start, end) => {
  const [x, y] = polar(r, start), [x2, y2] = polar(r, end);
  return `M ${x} ${y} A ${r} ${r} 0 ${end - start > 180 ? 1 : 0} 1 ${x2} ${y2}`;
};
const segment = (outer, inner, start, end) => {
  const a = polar(outer, start), b = polar(outer, end);
  const c = polar(inner, end), d = polar(inner, start);
  return `M ${a.join(' ')} A ${outer} ${outer} 0 0 1 ${b.join(' ')} L ${c.join(' ')} A ${inner} ${inner} 0 0 0 ${d.join(' ')} Z`;
};
const TICKS = Array.from({ length: 120 }, (_, i) => i);
const SECTORS = Array.from({ length: 12 }, (_, i) => i);

// Presentation only: no network, microphone, fabricated telemetry or call actions.
export default function Reactor({ doneToday = 0, pulseKey = 0 }) {
  const id = `jr-${useId().replace(/:/g, '')}`;
  const [paused, setPaused] = useState(false);
  const completed = Number.isFinite(doneToday) ? Math.max(0, Math.floor(doneToday)) : null;
  return (
    <section className={`jarvis-core${paused ? ' jarvis-core--paused' : ''}`} aria-label="Jarvis command core">
      <div className="jarvis-core__heading"><span className="jarvis-core__diamond" />PERSONAL INTELLIGENCE<span className="jarvis-core__rule" /><span>J / 01</span></div>
      <div className="jarvis-core__scene">
        <div className="jarvis-core__ambient" aria-hidden="true" />
        <svg className="jarvis-core__drawing" viewBox="0 0 640 640" fill="none" aria-hidden="true">
          <defs>
            <radialGradient id={`${id}-halo`}>
              <stop stopColor="#87f5ff" stopOpacity=".28" />
              <stop offset=".35" stopColor="#36cffa" stopOpacity=".09" />
              <stop offset="1" stopColor="#36cffa" stopOpacity="0" />
            </radialGradient>
            <radialGradient id={`${id}-glass`}>
              <stop stopColor="#1c718d" stopOpacity=".22" />
              <stop offset="1" stopColor="#020c1c" stopOpacity=".9" />
            </radialGradient>
            <linearGradient id={`${id}-metal`} x1="180" y1="120" x2="460" y2="510" gradientUnits="userSpaceOnUse">
              <stop stopColor="#defdff" /><stop offset=".42" stopColor="#459ab8" /><stop offset=".7" stopColor="#174563" /><stop offset="1" stopColor="#9becff" />
            </linearGradient>

              <stop stopColor="white" /><stop offset=".55" stopColor="#bcfaff" /><stop offset="1" stopColor="#43d5ff" />
            </linearGradient>
            <filter id={`${id}-bloom`} x="-80%" y="-80%" width="260%" height="260%">
              <feGaussianBlur stdDeviation="5" />
            </filter>
            <filter id={`${id}-soft`} x="-60%" y="-60%" width="220%" height="220%">
              <feGaussianBlur stdDeviation="1.8" />
            </filter>

          <circle cx="320" cy="320" r="275" fill={`url(#${id}-halo)`} />

          </g>

            {TICKS.map(i => {
              const major = i % 10 === 0, mid = i % 5 === 0;
              const a = polar(major ? 256 : mid ? 260 : 264, i * 3), b = polar(270, i * 3);
              return <path key={i} d={`M${a.join(' ')}L${b.join(' ')}`} strokeWidth={major ? 2 : 1} opacity={major ? .7 : mid ? .35 : .17} />;
            })}
          </g>
          <g className="jarvis-core__rotate jarvis-core__outer">
            {[0, 120, 240].map(deg => <g key={deg}>
              <path d={arc(244, deg + 7, deg + 76)} stroke={CYAN} strokeWidth="2" opacity=".6" />
              <path d={arc(239, deg + 7, deg + 48)} stroke={CYAN} strokeWidth=".7" opacity=".35" />

===== FILE: source/src/components/jarvis/StatColumn.jsx =====
import React from 'react';
import { useCountUp } from '@/hooks/useCountUp';
import { HUD } from '@/lib/jarvis';

function Stat({ value, label, gold, prefix }) {
  const display = useCountUp(value);
  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col">
      <span
        className="font-mono text-[40px] leading-none tabular-nums"
        style={{ color: gold ? HUD.amber : HUD.text }}
      >
        {prefix}{display}
      </span>
      <span className="font-mono text-[11px] uppercase tracking-[0.18em] text-hud-text2 mt-2">
        {label}
      </span>
    </div>
  );
}

export default function StatColumn({ revenue, spokenTo, leadsGenerated, tasksDone }) {
  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-8">
      <Stat value={revenue} label="Revenue This Month" gold prefix="$" />
      <Stat value={spokenTo} label="Businesses Spoken To" />
      <Stat value={leadsGenerated} label="Leads Generated" />
      <Stat value={tasksDone} label="Tasks Done" />
    </div>
  );
}

===== FILE: source/src/components/jarvis/TaskCard.jsx =====
import React from 'react';
import { Check, Loader2 } from 'lucide-react';
import { HUD, isOverdue, isFreshJarvisProgress, relTime } from '@/lib/jarvis';

const laneBorder = { doing: HUD.accent, todo: HUD.border, done: HUD.green };

export default function TaskCard({ task, lane, onAdvance, onComplete, saving, error, canEdit }) {
  const border = laneBorder[lane];
  const overdue = isOverdue(task.due_date);
  const busy = !!saving;
  const tickDisabled = busy || !canEdit;
  const freshProgress = isFreshJarvisProgress(task);

  return (
    <div
      className="px-5 py-4"
      style={{ borderLeft: `1px solid ${border}`, background: 'rgba(7,26,58,0.4)' }}
    >
      <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-3">
        <div className="min-w-0">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 flex-wrap">
            <span className="font-body text-[18px] text-hud-text break-words">{task.title || 'Untitled task'}</span>
            {task.priority === 'high' && (
              <span
                className="font-mono text-[11px] uppercase tracking-[0.1em] text-hud-amber px-2.5 py-1"
                style={{ border: '1px solid rgba(232,180,90,0.5)' }}
              >
                HIGH
              </span>
            )}
          </div>
          <div className="font-mono text-[14px] text-hud-faint mt-1">{task.priority}</div>
          {task.due_date && (
            <div className={`font-mono text-[14px] mt-0.5 ${overdue ? 'text-hud-red' : 'text-hud-faint'}`}>
              {task.due_date}
            </div>
          )}
          {(typeof task.notes === 'string' && task.notes.trim()) || (typeof task.result === 'string' && task.result.trim()) ? (
            <details className="mt-2">
              <summary className="font-mono text-[10px] uppercase tracking-[0.1em] text-hud-faint cursor-pointer">View result/details</summary>
              <div className="max-h-40 overflow-y-auto mt-1 font-body text-[14px] text-hud-text2/80 whitespace-pre-wrap break-words">
                {task.notes && <div>{task.notes}</div>}
                {task.result && <div className="text-hud-green/90 mt-2">{task.result}</div>}
              </div>
            </details>
          ) : null}
          {task.status === 'doing' && (
            <div className={`font-mono text-[10px] uppercase tracking-[0.1em] mt-2 ${freshProgress ? 'text-hud-accent' : 'text-hud-faint'}`} title="Based on latest saved agent progress; not a live worker heartbeat.">
              {task.execution_owner === 'jarvis'
                ? freshProgress ? 'Jarvis · In progress' : 'Jarvis · Awaiting progress update'
                : task.execution_owner === 'owner' ? 'Your task · In progress' : 'Board status · In progress'}
              {task.execution_owner === 'jarvis' && <span className="block mt-1">Updated {relTime(task.progress_updated_at)}</span>}
            </div>
          )}
          {typeof task.progress_note === 'string' && task.progress_note && (
            <p className="font-body text-[13px] text-hud-text2 mt-2 break-words">{task.progress_note}</p>
          )}
          {error && (
            <div className="font-mono text-[11px] tracking-[0.08em] text-hud-red mt-2">
              could not save
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
        <div className="shrink-0 mt-0.5 flex items-center gap-2">
          {task.status === 'todo' && (
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => !tickDisabled && onAdvance(task)}
              disabled={tickDisabled}
              className="font-mono text-[10px] uppercase tracking-[0.1em] text-hud-accent disabled:text-hud-faint"
              title={!canEdit ? 'sign in to edit' : 'Start as owner'}
            >Start</button>
          )}
          <button
            onClick={(e) => {
              e.stopPropagation();
              if (!tickDisabled) onComplete(task);
            }}
            disabled={tickDisabled}
            className={`${tickDisabled ? 'text-hud-faint' : 'text-hud-faint hover:text-hud-accent'}`}
            aria-label={task.status === 'done' ? 'Reopen task' : 'Mark done'}
          >
            {busy ? <Loader2 size={18} className="animate-spin" /> : <Check size={18} />}
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}


===== FILE: source/src/components/jarvis/TaskLanes.jsx =====
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import TaskCard from './TaskCard';
import { relTime } from '@/lib/jarvis';

const LANES = [
  { key: 'doing', label: 'Doing Now' },
  { key: 'todo', label: 'To Do' },
  { key: 'done', label: 'Done Today' }
];

export default function TaskLanes({ doingTasks, todoTasks, doneTasks, onAdvance, onComplete, onAdd, saving, errors, canEdit, activeJarvisTasks = [], activeStatus = 'ok', lastSyncAt }) {
  const [text, setText] = useState('');
  const data = { doing: doingTasks, todo: todoTasks, done: doneTasks };

  const submit = (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!canEdit) return;
    const t = text.trim();
    if (!t) return;
    onAdd(t);
    setText('');
  };

  return (
    <div>
      <div className="mb-5 px-4 py-3 font-mono text-[11px] uppercase tracking-[0.12em] text-hud-text2" style={{ border: '1px solid rgba(82,181,255,0.25)', background: 'rgba(7,26,58,0.35)' }}>
        {activeStatus === 'reconnecting'
          ? `RECONNECTING · ACTIVE COUNT HIDDEN · LAST SYNC ${lastSyncAt ? relTime(new Date(lastSyncAt).toISOString()) : 'unknown'}`
          : activeStatus === 'stale'
            ? `TASK SYNC STALE · ACTIVE COUNT HIDDEN · LAST SYNC ${lastSyncAt ? relTime(new Date(lastSyncAt).toISOString()) : 'unknown'}`
            : `JARVIS · ${activeJarvisTasks.length} task${activeJarvisTasks.length === 1 ? '' : 's'} in progress`}
        {activeStatus === 'ok' && <span className="ml-2 text-hud-faint normal-case" title="Based on latest saved agent progress; not a live worker heartbeat.">based on latest saved agent progress</span>}
        {activeStatus === 'ok' && activeJarvisTasks.slice(0, 3).map(task => (
          <div key={task.id} className="mt-2 font-body normal-case tracking-normal text-[14px] text-hud-accent break-words">
            {task.title}{task.progress_note ? ` — ${task.progress_note}` : ''}
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-3 gap-6">
      {LANES.map((lane) => (
        <div key={lane.key} className="flex flex-col">
          <div className="font-mono text-[13px] uppercase tracking-[0.15em] text-hud-text2 pb-2">
            {lane.label}
          </div>
          <div className="h-px bg-hud-border mb-3" />
          {lane.key === 'todo' && (
            <form onSubmit={submit} className="mb-3">
              <input
                value={text}
                onChange={(e) => setText(e.target.value)}
                placeholder={canEdit ? 'Add a task' : 'sign in to edit'}
                disabled={!canEdit}
                className="w-full bg-transparent border-0 border-b border-hud-border text-hud-text font-body py-1 outline-none placeholder:text-hud-faint disabled:opacity-60"
              />
            </form>
          )}
          <div className="flex flex-col gap-3">
            {data[lane.key].map((t) => (
              <TaskCard
                key={t.id}
                task={t}
                lane={lane.key}
                onAdvance={onAdvance}
                onComplete={onComplete}
                saving={!!saving[t.id]}
                error={!!errors[t.id]}
                canEdit={canEdit}
              />
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>
      ))}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}


===== FILE: source/src/components/ui/accordion.jsx =====
import * as React from "react"
import * as AccordionPrimitive from "@radix-ui/react-accordion"
import { ChevronDown } from "lucide-react"

import { cn } from "@/lib/utils"

const Accordion = AccordionPrimitive.Root

const AccordionItem = React.forwardRef(({ className, ...props }, ref) => (
  <AccordionPrimitive.Item ref={ref} className={cn("border-b", className)} {...props} />
))
AccordionItem.displayName = "AccordionItem"

const AccordionTrigger = React.forwardRef(({ className, children, ...props }, ref) => (
  <AccordionPrimitive.Header className="flex">
    <AccordionPrimitive.Trigger
      ref={ref}
      className={cn(
        "flex flex-1 items-center justify-between py-4 text-sm font-medium transition-all hover:underline text-left [&[data-state=open]>svg]:rotate-180",
        className
      )}
      {...props}>
      {children}
      <ChevronDown
        className="h-4 w-4 shrink-0 text-muted-foreground transition-transform duration-200" />
    </AccordionPrimitive.Trigger>
  </AccordionPrimitive.Header>
))
AccordionTrigger.displayName = AccordionPrimitive.Trigger.displayName

const AccordionContent = React.forwardRef(({ className, children, ...props }, ref) => (
  <AccordionPrimitive.Content
    ref={ref}
    className="overflow-hidden text-sm data-[state=closed]:animate-accordion-up data-[state=open]:animate-accordion-down"
    {...props}>
    <div className={cn("pb-4 pt-0", className)}>{children}</div>
  </AccordionPrimitive.Content>
))
AccordionContent.displayName = AccordionPrimitive.Content.displayName

export { Accordion, AccordionItem, AccordionTrigger, AccordionContent }


===== FILE: source/src/components/ui/alert-dialog.jsx =====
import * as React from "react"
import * as AlertDialogPrimitive from "@radix-ui/react-alert-dialog"

import { cn } from "@/lib/utils"
import { buttonVariants } from "@/components/ui/button"

const AlertDialog = AlertDialogPrimitive.Root

const AlertDialogTrigger = AlertDialogPrimitive.Trigger

const AlertDialogPortal = AlertDialogPrimitive.Portal

const AlertDialogOverlay = React.forwardRef(({ className, ...props }, ref) => (
  <AlertDialogPrimitive.Overlay
    className={cn(

import * as React from "react"
import { Slot } from "@radix-ui/react-slot"
import { cva } from "class-variance-authority";

import { cn } from "@/lib/utils"

const buttonVariants = cva(
  "inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-2 whitespace-nowrap rounded-md text-sm font-medium transition-colors focus-visible:outline-none focus-visible:ring-1 focus-visible:ring-ring disabled:pointer-events-none disabled:opacity-50 [&_svg]:pointer-events-none [&_svg]:size-4 [&_svg]:shrink-0",
  {
    variants: {
      variant: {
        default:
          "bg-primary text-primary-foreground shadow hover:bg-primary/90",
        destructive:
          "bg-destructive text-destructive-foreground shadow-sm hover:bg-destructive/90",
        outline:
          "border border-input bg-transparent shadow-sm hover:bg-accent hover:text-accent-foreground",
        secondary:
          "bg-secondary text-secondary-foreground shadow-sm hover:bg-secondary/80",
        ghost: "hover:bg-accent hover:text-accent-foreground",
        link: "text-primary underline-offset-4 hover:underline",
      },
      size: {
        default: "h-9 px-4 py-2",
        sm: "h-8 rounded-md px-3 text-xs",
        lg: "h-10 rounded-md px-8",
        icon: "h-9 w-9",
      },
    },
    defaultVariants: {
      variant: "default",
      size: "default",
    },
  }
)

const Button = React.forwardRef(({ className, variant, size, asChild = false, ...props }, ref) => {
  const Comp = asChild ? Slot : "button"

    (<Comp
      className={cn(buttonVariants({ variant, size, className }))}
      ref={ref}
      {...props} />)
  );
})

===== FILE: source/src/components/ui/carousel.jsx =====
import * as React from "react"
import useEmblaCarousel from "embla-carousel-react";
import { ArrowLeft, ArrowRight } from "lucide-react"

import { cn } from "@/lib/utils"
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button"

const CarouselContext = React.createContext(null)

function useCarousel() {
  const context = React.useContext(CarouselContext)

  if (!context) {
    throw new Error("useCarousel must be used within a <Carousel />")
  }

  return context
}

const Carousel = React.forwardRef((
  {
    orientation = "horizontal",
    opts,
    setApi,
    plugins,
    className,
    children,
    ...props
  },
  ref
) => {
  const [carouselRef, api] = useEmblaCarousel({
    ...opts,
    axis: orientation === "horizontal" ? "x" : "y",
  }, plugins)
  const [canScrollPrev, setCanScrollPrev] = React.useState(false)
  const [canScrollNext, setCanScrollNext] = React.useState(false)

  const onSelect = React.useCallback((api) => {
    if (!api) {
      return
    }

    setCanScrollPrev(api.canScrollPrev())
    setCanScrollNext(api.canScrollNext())
  }, [])

  const scrollPrev = React.useCallback(() => {
    api?.scrollPrev()
  }, [api])

  const scrollNext = React.useCallback(() => {
    api?.scrollNext()
  }, [api])

  const handleKeyDown = React.useCallback((event) => {
    if (event.key === "ArrowLeft") {
      event.preventDefault()
      scrollPrev()
    } else if (event.key === "ArrowRight") {
      event.preventDefault()
      scrollNext()
    }
  }, [scrollPrev, scrollNext])

  React.useEffect(() => {
    if (!api || !setApi) {
      return
    }

    setApi(api)
  }, [api, setApi])

  React.useEffect(() => {
    if (!api) {
      return
    }

    onSelect(api)
    api.on("reInit", onSelect)
    api.on("select", onSelect)

    return () => {
      api?.off("select", onSelect)
    };
  }, [api, onSelect])

  return (
    (<CarouselContext.Provider
      value={{
        carouselRef,
        api: api,
        opts,
        orientation:
          orientation || (opts?.axis === "y" ? "vertical" : "horizontal"),
        scrollPrev,
        scrollNext,
        canScrollPrev,
        canScrollNext,
      }}>
      <div
        ref={ref}
        onKeyDownCapture={handleKeyDown}
        className={cn("relative", className)}
        role="region"
        aria-roledescription="carousel"
        {...props}>
        {children}
      </div>
    </CarouselContext.Provider>)
  );
})
Carousel.displayName = "Carousel"

const CarouselContent = React.forwardRef(({ className, ...props }, ref) => {
  const { carouselRef, orientation } = useCarousel()

  return (
    (<div ref={carouselRef} className="overflow-hidden">
      <div
        ref={ref}
        className={cn(
          "flex",
          orientation === "horizontal" ? "-ml-4" : "-mt-4 flex-col",
          className
        )}
        {...props} />
    </div>)
  );
})
CarouselContent.displayName = "CarouselContent"

const CarouselItem = React.forwardRef(({ className, ...props }, ref) => {
  const { orientation } = useCarousel()

  return (
    (<div
      ref={ref}
      role="group"
      aria-roledescription="slide"
      className={cn(
        "min-w-0 shrink-0 grow-0 basis-full",
        orientation === "horizontal" ? "pl-4" : "pt-4",
        className
      )}
      {...props} />)
  );
})
CarouselItem.displayName = "CarouselItem"

const CarouselPrevious = React.forwardRef(({ className, variant = "outline", size = "icon", ...props }, ref) => {
  const { orientation, scrollPrev, canScrollPrev } = useCarousel()

  return (
    (<Button
      ref={ref}
      variant={variant}
      size={size}
      className={cn("absolute  h-8 w-8 rounded-full", orientation === "horizontal"
        ? "-left-12 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2"
        : "-top-12 left-1/2 -translate-x-1/2 rotate-90", className)}
      disabled={!canScrollPrev}
      onClick={scrollPrev}
      {...props}>
      <ArrowLeft className="h-4 w-4" />
      <span className="sr-only">Previous slide</span>
    </Button>)
  );
})
CarouselPrevious.displayName = "CarouselPrevious"

const CarouselNext = React.forwardRef(({ className, variant = "outline", size = "icon", ...props }, ref) => {
  const { orientation, scrollNext, canScrollNext } = useCarousel()

  return (
    (<Button
      ref={ref}
      variant={variant}
      size={size}
      className={cn("absolute h-8 w-8 rounded-full", orientation === "horizontal"
        ? "-right-12 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2"
        : "-bottom-12 left-1/2 -translate-x-1/2 rotate-90", className)}
      disabled={!canScrollNext}
      onClick={scrollNext}
      {...props}>
      <ArrowRight className="h-4 w-4" />
      <span className="sr-only">Next slide</span>
    </Button>)
  );
})
CarouselNext.displayName = "CarouselNext"

export { Carousel, CarouselContent, CarouselItem, CarouselPrevious, CarouselNext };


===== FILE: source/src/components/ui/chart.jsx =====
"use client";
import * as React from "react"
import * as RechartsPrimitive from "recharts"

import { cn } from "@/lib/utils"

// Format: { THEME_NAME: CSS_SELECTOR }
const THEMES = {
  light: "",
  dark: ".dark"
}

const ChartContext = React.createContext(null)

function useChart() {
  const context = React.useContext(ChartContext)

  if (!context) {
    throw new Error("useChart must be used within a <ChartContainer />")
  }

  return context
}

const ChartContainer = React.forwardRef(({ id, className, children, config, ...props }, ref) => {
  const uniqueId = React.useId()
  const chartId = `chart-${id || uniqueId.replace(/:/g, "")}`

  return (
    (<ChartContext.Provider value={{ config }}>
      <div
        data-chart={chartId}
        ref={ref}
        className={cn(
          "flex aspect-video justify-center text-xs [&_.recharts-cartesian-axis-tick_text]:fill-muted-foreground [&_.recharts-cartesian-grid_line[stroke='#ccc']]:stroke-border/50 [&_.recharts-curve.recharts-tooltip-cursor]:stroke-border [&_.recharts-dot[stroke='#fff']]:stroke-transparent [&_.recharts-layer]:outline-none [&_.recharts-polar-grid_[stroke='#ccc']]:stroke-border [&_.recharts-radial-bar-background-sector]:fill-muted [&_.recharts-rectangle.recharts-tooltip-cursor]:fill-muted [&_.recharts-reference-line_[stroke='#ccc']]:stroke-border [&_.recharts-sector[stroke='#fff']]:stroke-transparent [&_.recharts-sector]:outline-none [&_.recharts-surface]:outline-none",
          className
        )}
        {...props}>
        <ChartStyle id={chartId} config={config} />
        <RechartsPrimitive.ResponsiveContainer>
          {children}
        </RechartsPrimitive.ResponsiveContainer>
      </div>
    </ChartContext.Provider>)
  );
})
ChartContainer.displayName = "Chart"

const ChartStyle = ({
  id,
  config
}) => {
  const colorConfig = Object.entries(config).filter(([, config]) => config.theme || config.color)

  if (!colorConfig.length) {
    return null
  }

  return (
    (<style
      dangerouslySetInnerHTML={{
        __html: Object.entries(THEMES)
          .map(([theme, prefix]) => `
${prefix} [data-chart=${id}] {
${colorConfig
.map(([key, itemConfig]) => {
const color =
  itemConfig.theme?.[theme] ||
  itemConfig.color
return color ? `  --color-${key}: ${color};` : null
})
.join("\n")}
}
`)
          .join("\n"),
      }} />)
  );
}

const ChartTooltip = RechartsPrimitive.Tooltip

const ChartTooltipContent = React.forwardRef((
  {
    active,
    payload,
    className,
    indicator = "dot",
    hideLabel = false,
    hideIndicator = false,
    label,
    labelFormatter,
    labelClassName,
    formatter,
    color,
    nameKey,
    labelKey,
  },
  ref
) => {
  const { config } = useChart()

  const tooltipLabel = React.useMemo(() => {
    if (hideLabel || !payload?.length) {
      return null
    }

    const [item] = payload
    const key = `${labelKey || item.dataKey || item.name || "value"}`
    const itemConfig = getPayloadConfigFromPayload(config, item, key)
    const value =
      !labelKey && typeof label === "string"
        ? config[label]?.label || label
        : itemConfig?.label

    if (labelFormatter) {
      return (
        (<div className={cn("font-medium", labelClassName)}>
          {labelFormatter(value, payload)}
        </div>)
      );
    }

    if (!value) {
      return null
    }

    return <div className={cn("font-medium", labelClassName)}>{value}</div>;
  }, [
    label,
    labelFormatter,
    payload,
    hideLabel,
    labelClassName,
    config,
    labelKey,
  ])

  if (!active || !payload?.length) {
    return null
  }

  const nestLabel = payload.length === 1 && indicator !== "dot"

  return (
    (<div
      ref={ref}
      className={cn(
        "grid min-w-[8rem] items-start gap-1.5 rounded-lg border border-border/50 bg-background px-2.5 py-1.5 text-xs shadow-xl",
        className
      )}>
      {!nestLabel ? tooltipLabel : null}
      <div className="grid gap-1.5">
        {payload.map((item, index) => {
          const key = `${nameKey || item.name || item.dataKey || "value"}`
          const itemConfig = getPayloadConfigFromPayload(config, item, key)
          const indicatorColor = color || item.payload.fill || item.color

          return (
            (<div
              key={item.dataKey}
              className={cn(
                "flex w-full flex-wrap items-stretch gap-2 [&>svg]:h-2.5 [&>svg]:w-2.5 [&>svg]:text-muted-foreground",
                indicator === "dot" && "items-center"
              )}>
              {formatter && item?.value !== undefined && item.name ? (
                formatter(item.value, item.name, item, index, item.payload)
              ) : (
                <>
                  {itemConfig?.icon ? (
                    <itemConfig.icon />
                  ) : (
                    !hideIndicator && (
                      <div
                        className={cn("shrink-0 rounded-[2px] border-[--color-border] bg-[--color-bg]", {
                          "h-2.5 w-2.5": indicator === "dot",
                          "w-1": indicator === "line",
                          "w-0 border-[1.5px] border-dashed bg-transparent":
                            indicator === "dashed",
                          "my-0.5": nestLabel && indicator === "dashed",
                        })}
                        style={
                          {
                            "--color-bg": indicatorColor,
                            "--color-border": indicatorColor
                          }
                        } />
                    )
                  )}
                  <div
                    className={cn(
                      "flex flex-1 justify-between leading-none",
                      nestLabel ? "items-end" : "items-center"
                    )}>
                    <div className="grid gap-1.5">
                      {nestLabel ? tooltipLabel : null}
                      <span className="text-muted-foreground">
                        {itemConfig?.label || item.name}
                      </span>
                    </div>
                    {item.value && (
                      <span className="font-mono font-medium tabular-nums text-foreground">
                        {item.value.toLocaleString()}
                      </span>
                    )}
                  </div>
                </>
              )}
            </div>)
          );
        })}
      </div>
    </div>)
  );
})
ChartTooltipContent.displayName = "ChartTooltip"

const ChartLegend = RechartsPrimitive.Legend

const ChartLegendContent = React.forwardRef((
  { className, hideIcon = false, payload, verticalAlign = "bottom", nameKey },
  ref
) => {
  const { config } = useChart()

  if (!payload?.length) {
    return null
  }

  return (
    (<div
      ref={ref}
      className={cn(
        "flex items-center justify-center gap-4",
        verticalAlign === "top" ? "pb-3" : "pt-3",
        className
      )}>
      {payload.map((item) => {
        const key = `${nameKey || item.dataKey || "value"}`
        const itemConfig = getPayloadConfigFromPayload(config, item, key)

        return (
          (<div
            key={item.value}
            className={cn(
              "flex items-center gap-1.5 [&>svg]:h-3 [&>svg]:w-3 [&>svg]:text-muted-foreground"
            )}>
            {itemConfig?.icon && !hideIcon ? (
              <itemConfig.icon />
            ) : (
              <div
                className="h-2 w-2 shrink-0 rounded-[2px]"
                style={{
                  backgroundColor: item.color,
                }} />
            )}
            {itemConfig?.label}
          </div>)
        );
      })}
    </div>)
  );
})
ChartLegendContent.displayName = "ChartLegend"

// Helper to extract item config from a payload.
function getPayloadConfigFromPayload(
  config,
  payload,
  key
) {
  if (typeof payload !== "object" || payload === null) {
    return undefined
  }

  const payloadPayload =
    "payload" in payload &&
    typeof payload.payload === "object" &&
    payload.payload !== null
      ? payload.payload
      : undefined

  let configLabelKey = key

  if (
    key in payload &&
    typeof payload[key] === "string"
  ) {
    configLabelKey = payload[key]
  } else if (
    payloadPayload &&
    key in payloadPayload &&
    typeof payloadPayload[key] === "string"
  ) {
    configLabelKey = payloadPayload[key]
  }

  return configLabelKey in config
    ? config[configLabelKey]
    : config[key];
}

export {
  ChartContainer,
  ChartTooltip,
  ChartTooltipContent,
  ChartLegend,
  ChartLegendContent,
  ChartStyle,
}

===== FILE: source/src/components/ui/form.jsx =====
"use client";
import * as React from "react"
import { Slot } from "@radix-ui/react-slot"
import { Controller, FormProvider, useFormContext } from "react-hook-form";

import { cn } from "@/lib/utils"
import { Label } from "@/components/ui/label"

const Form = FormProvider

const FormFieldContext = React.createContext({})

const FormField = (
  {
    ...props
  }
) => {
  return (
    (<FormFieldContext.Provider value={{ name: props.name }}>
      <Controller {...props} />
    </FormFieldContext.Provider>)
  );
}

const useFormField = () => {
  const fieldContext = React.useContext(FormFieldContext)
  const itemContext = React.useContext(FormItemContext)
  const { getFieldState, formState } = useFormContext()

  const fieldState = getFieldState(fieldContext.name, formState)

  if (!fieldContext) {
    throw new Error("useFormField should be used within <FormField>")
  }

  const { id } = itemContext

  return {
    id,
    name: fieldContext.name,
    formItemId: `${id}-form-item`,
    formDescriptionId: `${id}-form-item-description`,
    formMessageId: `${id}-form-item-message`,
    ...fieldState,
  }
}

const FormItemContext = React.createContext({})

const FormItem = React.forwardRef(({ className, ...props }, ref) => {
  const id = React.useId()

  return (
    (<FormItemContext.Provider value={{ id }}>
      <div ref={ref} className={cn("space-y-2", className)} {...props} />
    </FormItemContext.Provider>)
  );
})
FormItem.displayName = "FormItem"

const FormLabel = React.forwardRef(({ className, ...props }, ref) => {
  const { error, formItemId } = useFormField()

  return (
    (<Label
      ref={ref}
      className={cn(error && "text-destructive", className)}
      htmlFor={formItemId}
      {...props} />)
  );
})
FormLabel.displayName = "FormLabel"

const FormControl = React.forwardRef(({ ...props }, ref) => {
  const { error, formItemId, formDescriptionId, formMessageId } = useFormField()

  return (
    (<Slot
      ref={ref}
      id={formItemId}
      aria-describedby={
        !error
          ? `${formDescriptionId}`
          : `${formDescriptionId} ${formMessageId}`
      }
      aria-invalid={!!error}
      {...props} />)
  );
})
FormControl.displayName = "FormControl"

const FormDescription = React.forwardRef(({ className, ...props }, ref) => {
  const { formDescriptionId } = useFormField()

  return (
    (<p
      ref={ref}
      id={formDescriptionId}
      className={cn("text-[0.8rem] text-muted-foreground", className)}
      {...props} />)
  );
})
FormDescription.displayName = "FormDescription"

const FormMessage = React.forwardRef(({ className, children, ...props }, ref) => {
  const { error, formMessageId } = useFormField()
  const body = error ? String(error?.message) : children

  if (!body) {
    return null
  }

  return (
    (<p
      ref={ref}
      id={formMessageId}
      className={cn("text-[0.8rem] font-medium text-destructive", className)}
      {...props}>
      {body}
    </p>)
  );
})
FormMessage.displayName = "FormMessage"

export {
  useFormField,
  Form,
  FormItem,
  FormLabel,
  FormControl,
  FormDescription,
  FormMessage,
  FormField,
}
